    # ============================================================================
    # PUBLICATION-READY WEAR FIGURES — MSSP / RESS / Elsevier (FINAL UPDATED v10)
    # Phase labels: smaller, lighter (less dominant)
    # Bottom panel: UNIFIED -> Predictive uncertainty (±2σ) for ALL SIX figures
    #               (validation C1/C4/C6 AND test C2/C3/C5)
    # ============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import matplotlib.font_manager as fm
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch, Rectangle
    from matplotlib.transforms import blended_transform_factory

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- paths
    RESULTS_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real"
    TRAJ_DIR    = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, "trajectories")
    FIG_DIR     = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, "figures_wear_MSSP_RESS")
    os.makedirs(FIG_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    VAL_FILE  = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    TEST_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_test.csv")

    DPI = 600

    # ---- design switches --------------------------------------------------------
    SHOW_BASELINE_BANDS = False
    BAND_ABLATION       = False
    FONT_STYLE           = "serif"
    FIGSIZE_WITH_PANEL   = (5.6, 5.9)

    BAND_ALPHA           = 0.30

    LW_BASELINE = 0.75
    LW_PROPOSED = 2.0
    LW_TRUTH    = 1.4
    MS_TRUTH    = 4
    GT_MARKEVERY = 12

    ALPHA_BASELINE = 0.95
    ALPHA_PROPOSED = 1.0

    FAILURE_THRESHOLD_UM = 165.0  # VERIFY against your cited source

    PHASE_FRACTIONS = {"early_end": 0.12, "mid_end": 0.80}

    PHASE_LABEL_Y = {
        "Break-in":         0.62,
        "Steady wear":      0.035,
        "Accelerated wear": 0.40,
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- load data
    val_df  = pd.read_csv(VAL_FILE)
    test_df = pd.read_csv(TEST_FILE)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- model mapping
    ROLE = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed",
        "Proposed": "PINN",
    }
    baselines   = ["TCN", "BiLSTM", "Transformer", "Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
    all_methods = baselines + ["Proposed"]

    DISPLAY = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Deep State Space Model",
        "Proposed": "Proposed",
    }

    def wcol(label): return f"{ROLE[label]}_wear"
    def scol(label): return f"{ROLE[label]}_wear_std"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- palette
    COLORS = {
        "Ground truth":            "#2B2B2B",
        "TCN":                     "#8E7CC3",
        "BiLSTM":                  "#4FA88B",
        "Transformer":             "#4F8FC0",
        "Neural ODE":              "#E2895B",
        "Deep State Space Model":  "#9A9A9A",
        "Proposed":                "#D7263D",
    }

    PHASE_COLORS = {
        "Break-in":         "#F2E9DC",
        "Steady wear":      "#E9F1EC",
        "Accelerated wear": "#F7E4E4",
    }
    PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA = 0.48

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- fonts
    _avail = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    if FONT_STYLE == "serif":
        _family = next((f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in _avail), "serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.serif"]  = [_family]
    else:
        _family = next((f for f in ["Arial", "Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans"] if f in _avail), "sans-serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "sans-serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.sans-serif"] = [_family]

    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 8,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.0,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.width": 1.0, "ytick.major.width": 1.0,
        "xtick.minor.width": 0.7, "ytick.minor.width": 0.7,
        "xtick.major.size": 4,    "ytick.major.size": 4,
        "xtick.minor.size": 2,    "ytick.minor.size": 2,
        "lines.linewidth": 1.2,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42, "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
    def metrics(y_true, y_pred):
        y_true, y_pred = np.asarray(y_true), np.asarray(y_pred)
        rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_pred - y_true) ** 2))
        mae  = np.mean(np.abs(y_pred - y_true))
        r2   = 1 - np.sum((y_true - y_pred) ** 2) / np.sum((y_true - y_true.mean()) ** 2)
        return rmse, mae, r2

    def style_main_axis(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both", labelbottom=False)
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_ylabel("Flank wear ($\\mu$m)")

    def style_bottom_axis(ax, ylabel):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_xlabel("Cutting pass")
        ax.set_ylabel(ylabel)

    def cutter_tag(ax, name):
        ax.text(0.95, 0.07, name, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=11,
                fontweight="bold", ha="right", va="bottom",
                bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.25", facecolor="white",
                          edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.95))

    def save_fig(fig, name):
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, f"{name}.png"),
                    dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")

    def add_phase_shading(ax, x):
        """Break-in / Steady wear / Accelerated wear shading, left -> right."""
        n = len(x)
        i_early = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["early_end"])
        i_mid   = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["mid_end"])

        x_early_end = x[i_early] if i_early < n else x[-1]
        x_mid_end   = x[i_mid]   if i_mid   < n else x[-1]

        ax.axvspan(x[0], x_early_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Break-in"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_early_end, x_mid_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Steady wear"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_mid_end, x[-1], color=PHASE_COLORS["Accelerated wear"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)

        trans = blended_transform_factory(ax.transData, ax.transAxes)
        for (xa, xb, label) in [
            (x[0], x_early_end, "Break-in"),
            (x_early_end, x_mid_end, "Steady wear"),
            (x_mid_end, x[-1], "Accelerated wear"),
        ]:
            xc = (xa + xb) / 2
            y_frac = PHASE_LABEL_Y[label]
            # smaller, lighter, non-bold -> less visually dominant than the data
            ax.text(xc, y_frac, label, transform=trans, ha="center", va="center",
                    fontsize=6.8, style="italic", fontweight="normal",
                    color="#8A8A8A", zorder=30, clip_on=True)

    def add_failure_threshold(ax):
        ax.axhline(FAILURE_THRESHOLD_UM, color="#7A0C0C", linestyle="--",
                   linewidth=1.1, alpha=0.85, zorder=15)
        xmin, xmax = ax.get_xlim()
        ymin, ymax = ax.get_ylim()
        x_txt = xmin + 0.5 * (xmax - xmin)
        if FAILURE_THRESHOLD_UM > ymin + 0.85 * (ymax - ymin):
            y_txt, va = FAILURE_THRESHOLD_UM - 0.03 * (ymax - ymin), "top"
        else:
            y_txt, va = FAILURE_THRESHOLD_UM + 0.03 * (ymax - ymin), "bottom"
        ax.text(x_txt, y_txt, "Failure threshold", fontsize=8, color="#7A0C0C",
                ha="center", va=va, fontweight="bold", clip_on=True, zorder=25)

    def draw_method(ax, d, x, method, banded):
        y = d[wcol(method)].to_numpy()
        s = d[scol(method)].to_numpy()
        is_prop = method == "Proposed"
        ax.plot(x, y, linestyle="-", color=COLORS[method],
                linewidth=LW_PROPOSED if is_prop else LW_BASELINE,
                alpha=ALPHA_PROPOSED if is_prop else ALPHA_BASELINE,
                zorder=20 if is_prop else 3,
                label=DISPLAY[method])
        if banded:
            lo = np.maximum(y - 2 * s, 0)
            hi = y + 2 * s
            a = BAND_ALPHA if is_prop else BAND_ALPHA * 0.55
            ax.fill_between(x, lo, hi, color=COLORS[method], alpha=a,
                            linewidth=0, zorder=18 if is_prop else 1)
            if is_prop:
                ax.plot(x, lo, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)
                ax.plot(x, hi, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)

    def make_handles(include_truth):
        h = []
        if include_truth:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Ground truth"], lw=LW_TRUTH,
                            marker="o", markerfacecolor="white",
                            markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                            markeredgewidth=0.9, markersize=5.5, label="Ground truth"))
        for m in baselines:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS[m], lw=1.2, linestyle="-",
                            label=DISPLAY[m]))
        h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=2.0,
                        label=DISPLAY["Proposed"]))
        h.append(Patch(facecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=BAND_ALPHA,
                       edgecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], linewidth=0.5,
                       label="Proposed $\\pm2\\sigma$"))
        h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color="#7A0C0C", lw=1.1, linestyle="--",
                        label="Failure threshold"))
        return h

    def make_cutter_figure(df, cutter, with_truth, out_name):
        d = df[df["cutter"] == cutter].sort_values("cut_number")
        if d.empty:
            print(f"No data for {cutter}")
            return
        x = d["cut_number"].to_numpy()

        fig = plt.figure(figsize=FIGSIZE_WITH_PANEL)
        gs = fig.add_gridspec(3, 1, height_ratios=[0.75, 3, 1], hspace=0.10)
        ax_legend = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])
        ax        = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])
        ax_bottom = fig.add_subplot(gs[2], sharex=ax)

        ax_legend.axis("off")
        ax_legend.set_xlim(0, 1)
        ax_legend.set_ylim(0, 1)

        for m in baselines:
            band = SHOW_BASELINE_BANDS or (BAND_ABLATION and m == "Deep State Space Model")
            draw_method(ax, d, x, m, banded=band)

        if with_truth:
            ax.plot(x, d["wear_true"].to_numpy(), "o-", color=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                    linewidth=LW_TRUTH, markersize=MS_TRUTH, markerfacecolor="white",
                    markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"], markeredgewidth=0.9,
                    markevery=GT_MARKEVERY,
                    zorder=12, label="Ground truth")

        draw_method(ax, d, x, "Proposed", banded=True)

        add_phase_shading(ax, x)
        add_failure_threshold(ax)

        style_main_axis(ax)
        cutter_tag(ax, cutter.upper())

        rect = Rectangle((0, 0), 1, 1, transform=ax_legend.transAxes,
                         facecolor="white", edgecolor="black", linewidth=1.3,
                         joinstyle="miter", zorder=0)
        ax_legend.add_patch(rect)

        handles = make_handles(with_truth)
        legend = ax_legend.legend(handles=handles, loc="center",
                                  frameon=False,
                                  ncol=3, handlelength=1.6,
                                  columnspacing=0.9,
                                  labelspacing=0.9,
                                  borderpad=1.4,
                                  handletextpad=0.5)
        for t in legend.get_texts():
            t.set_fontweight("bold")

        # ---- bottom subpanel: UNIFIED across all six -> predictive uncertainty ----
        # (previously validation cutters showed prediction error since ground truth
        # exists there; now all six show the same ±2σ uncertainty panel for
        # visual/narrative consistency across the figure set, per your request)
        y = d[wcol("Proposed")].to_numpy()
        s = d[scol("Proposed")].to_numpy()
        ax_bottom.fill_between(x, -2 * s, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=0.30, linewidth=0)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, -2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.axhline(0, color="black", lw=0.8, alpha=0.6)
        style_bottom_axis(ax_bottom, "Uncertainty ($\\mu$m)")

        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, out_name)
        plt.close(fig)
        print(f"Saved {cutter.upper()} -> {out_name}")

    # ============================================================================
    # VALIDATION CUTTERS (with ground truth): C1, C4, C6
    # ============================================================================
    for cutter in ["c1", "c4", "c6"]:
        make_cutter_figure(val_df, cutter, with_truth=True,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_Wear_Validation_MSSP_RESS")

    # ============================================================================
    # TEST CUTTERS (predictions only, no ground truth exists): C2, C3, C5
    # ============================================================================
    for cutter in ["c2", "c3", "c5"]:
        make_cutter_figure(test_df, cutter, with_truth=False,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_Wear_Test_MSSP_RESS")

    # ============================================================================
    # METRICS TABLE (validation only)
    # ============================================================================
    mask = val_df["wear_true"].notna()
    y_true_all = val_df.loc[mask, "wear_true"].to_numpy()

    rows = []
    for method in all_methods:
        rmse, mae, r2 = metrics(y_true_all, val_df.loc[mask, wcol(method)].to_numpy())
        cat = ("Proposed" if method == "Proposed"
               else "Ablation" if method in ["Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
               else "Baseline")
        rows.append({"Method": DISPLAY[method], "Category": cat,
                     "RMSE": rmse, "MAE": mae, "R2": r2})

    metric_df = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("RMSE").reset_index(drop=True)
    metric_df.to_csv(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, "Wear_Metrics_MSSP_RESS.csv"), index=False)

    print("\nWear validation metrics:")
    print(metric_df.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nLaTeX table rows:")
    for _, r in metric_df.iterrows():
        bold = r["Method"] == "Proposed"
        fmt = (lambda v: f"\\textbf{{{v}}}") if bold else (lambda v: f"{v}")
        print(f"{fmt(r['Method'])} & {fmt(r['Category'])} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['RMSE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['MAE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['R2'], '.4f'))} \\\\")

    print(f"\nAll figures and metrics saved in:\n{FIG_DIR}")

    Neural ODE & Ablation & 15.84 & 12.74 & 0.8217 \\

    # ============================================================================
    # PUBLICATION-READY WEAR FIGURES — MSSP / RESS / Elsevier (FINAL UPDATED v12)
    # Failure threshold: REAL per-cutter value for C1/C4/C6 (from actual
    # wear_true). Removed entirely for C2/C3/C5 -- no ground truth exists for
    # those cutters, so there is no basis for any threshold line, real or nominal.
    # ============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import matplotlib.font_manager as fm
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch, Rectangle
    from matplotlib.transforms import blended_transform_factory

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- paths
    RESULTS_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real"
    TRAJ_DIR    = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, "trajectories")
    FIG_DIR     = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, "figures_wear_MSSP_RESS")
    os.makedirs(FIG_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    VAL_FILE  = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    TEST_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_test.csv")

    DPI = 600

    # ---- design switches --------------------------------------------------------
    SHOW_BASELINE_BANDS = False
    BAND_ABLATION       = False
    FONT_STYLE           = "serif"
    FIGSIZE_WITH_PANEL   = (5.6, 5.9)

    BAND_ALPHA           = 0.30

    LW_BASELINE = 0.75
    LW_PROPOSED = 2.0
    LW_TRUTH    = 1.4
    MS_TRUTH    = 4
    GT_MARKEVERY = 12

    ALPHA_BASELINE = 0.95
    ALPHA_PROPOSED = 1.0

    # ---- Failure threshold: REAL, per-cutter, validation only ------------------
    # Filled in automatically below from the CSV's actual final wear_true value.
    # No threshold is drawn for test cutters (C2/C3/C5) since no ground truth
    # exists for them -- see make_cutter_figure().
    FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER = {}

    PHASE_FRACTIONS = {"early_end": 0.12, "mid_end": 0.80}

    PHASE_LABEL_Y = {
        "Break-in":         0.62,
        "Steady wear":      0.035,
        "Accelerated wear": 0.40,
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- load data
    val_df  = pd.read_csv(VAL_FILE)
    test_df = pd.read_csv(TEST_FILE)

    for _c in ["c1", "c4", "c6"]:
        _d = val_df[val_df["cutter"] == _c].sort_values("cut_number")
        if not _d.empty and _d["wear_true"].notna().any():
            FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER[_c] = float(_d["wear_true"].dropna().iloc[-1])

    print("Per-cutter failure thresholds (from actual final measured wear):")
    for k, v in FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER.items():
        print(f"  {k.upper()}: {v:.2f} um")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- model mapping
    ROLE = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed",
        "Proposed": "PINN",
    }
    baselines   = ["TCN", "BiLSTM", "Transformer", "Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
    all_methods = baselines + ["Proposed"]

    DISPLAY = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Deep State Space Model",
        "Proposed": "Proposed",
    }

    def wcol(label): return f"{ROLE[label]}_wear"
    def scol(label): return f"{ROLE[label]}_wear_std"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- palette
    COLORS = {
        "Ground truth":            "#2B2B2B",
        "TCN":                     "#8E7CC3",
        "BiLSTM":                  "#4FA88B",
        "Transformer":             "#4F8FC0",
        "Neural ODE":              "#E2895B",
        "Deep State Space Model":  "#9A9A9A",
        "Proposed":                "#D7263D",
    }

    PHASE_COLORS = {
        "Break-in":         "#F2E9DC",
        "Steady wear":      "#E9F1EC",
        "Accelerated wear": "#F7E4E4",
    }
    PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA = 0.48

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- fonts
    _avail = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    if FONT_STYLE == "serif":
        _family = next((f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in _avail), "serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.serif"]  = [_family]
    else:
        _family = next((f for f in ["Arial", "Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans"] if f in _avail), "sans-serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "sans-serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.sans-serif"] = [_family]

    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 8,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.0,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.width": 1.0, "ytick.major.width": 1.0,
        "xtick.minor.width": 0.7, "ytick.minor.width": 0.7,
        "xtick.major.size": 4,    "ytick.major.size": 4,
        "xtick.minor.size": 2,    "ytick.minor.size": 2,
        "lines.linewidth": 1.2,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42, "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
    def metrics(y_true, y_pred):
        y_true, y_pred = np.asarray(y_true), np.asarray(y_pred)
        rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_pred - y_true) ** 2))
        mae  = np.mean(np.abs(y_pred - y_true))
        r2   = 1 - np.sum((y_true - y_pred) ** 2) / np.sum((y_true - y_true.mean()) ** 2)
        return rmse, mae, r2

    def style_main_axis(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both", labelbottom=False)
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_ylabel("Flank wear ($\\mu$m)")

    def style_bottom_axis(ax, ylabel):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_xlabel("Cutting pass")
        ax.set_ylabel(ylabel)

    def cutter_tag(ax, name):
        ax.text(0.95, 0.07, name, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=11,
                fontweight="bold", ha="right", va="bottom",
                bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.25", facecolor="white",
                          edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.95))

    def save_fig(fig, name):
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, f"{name}.png"),
                    dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")

    def add_phase_shading(ax, x):
        n = len(x)
        i_early = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["early_end"])
        i_mid   = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["mid_end"])

        x_early_end = x[i_early] if i_early < n else x[-1]
        x_mid_end   = x[i_mid]   if i_mid   < n else x[-1]

        ax.axvspan(x[0], x_early_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Break-in"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_early_end, x_mid_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Steady wear"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_mid_end, x[-1], color=PHASE_COLORS["Accelerated wear"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)

        trans = blended_transform_factory(ax.transData, ax.transAxes)
        for (xa, xb, label) in [
            (x[0], x_early_end, "Break-in"),
            (x_early_end, x_mid_end, "Steady wear"),
            (x_mid_end, x[-1], "Accelerated wear"),
        ]:
            xc = (xa + xb) / 2
            y_frac = PHASE_LABEL_Y[label]
            ax.text(xc, y_frac, label, transform=trans, ha="center", va="center",
                    fontsize=6.8, style="italic", fontweight="normal",
                    color="#8A8A8A", zorder=30, clip_on=True)

    def add_failure_threshold(ax, threshold_value):
        """Only called for validation cutters (real ground-truth termination value)."""
        ax.axhline(threshold_value, color="#7A0C0C", linestyle="--",
                   linewidth=1.1, alpha=0.85, zorder=15)
        xmin, xmax = ax.get_xlim()
        ymin, ymax = ax.get_ylim()
        x_txt = xmin + 0.5 * (xmax - xmin)
        if threshold_value > ymin + 0.85 * (ymax - ymin):
            y_txt, va = threshold_value - 0.03 * (ymax - ymin), "top"
        else:
            y_txt, va = threshold_value + 0.03 * (ymax - ymin), "bottom"
        ax.text(x_txt, y_txt, "Failure threshold", fontsize=8, color="#7A0C0C",
                ha="center", va=va, fontweight="bold", clip_on=True, zorder=25)

    def draw_method(ax, d, x, method, banded):
        y = d[wcol(method)].to_numpy()
        s = d[scol(method)].to_numpy()
        is_prop = method == "Proposed"
        ax.plot(x, y, linestyle="-", color=COLORS[method],
                linewidth=LW_PROPOSED if is_prop else LW_BASELINE,
                alpha=ALPHA_PROPOSED if is_prop else ALPHA_BASELINE,
                zorder=20 if is_prop else 3,
                label=DISPLAY[method])
        if banded:
            lo = np.maximum(y - 2 * s, 0)
            hi = y + 2 * s
            a = BAND_ALPHA if is_prop else BAND_ALPHA * 0.55
            ax.fill_between(x, lo, hi, color=COLORS[method], alpha=a,
                            linewidth=0, zorder=18 if is_prop else 1)
            if is_prop:
                ax.plot(x, lo, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)
                ax.plot(x, hi, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)

    def make_handles(include_truth, include_threshold):
        h = []
        if include_truth:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Ground truth"], lw=LW_TRUTH,
                            marker="o", markerfacecolor="white",
                            markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                            markeredgewidth=0.9, markersize=5.5, label="Ground truth"))
        for m in baselines:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS[m], lw=1.2, linestyle="-",
                            label=DISPLAY[m]))
        h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=2.0,
                        label=DISPLAY["Proposed"]))
        h.append(Patch(facecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=BAND_ALPHA,
                       edgecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], linewidth=0.5,
                       label="Proposed $\\pm2\\sigma$"))
        if include_threshold:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color="#7A0C0C", lw=1.1, linestyle="--",
                            label="Failure threshold"))
        return h

    def make_cutter_figure(df, cutter, with_truth, out_name):
        d = df[df["cutter"] == cutter].sort_values("cut_number")
        if d.empty:
            print(f"No data for {cutter}")
            return
        x = d["cut_number"].to_numpy()

        has_threshold = cutter in FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER   # True only for C1/C4/C6

        fig = plt.figure(figsize=FIGSIZE_WITH_PANEL)
        gs = fig.add_gridspec(3, 1, height_ratios=[0.75, 3, 1], hspace=0.10)
        ax_legend = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])
        ax        = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])
        ax_bottom = fig.add_subplot(gs[2], sharex=ax)

        ax_legend.axis("off")
        ax_legend.set_xlim(0, 1)
        ax_legend.set_ylim(0, 1)

        for m in baselines:
            band = SHOW_BASELINE_BANDS or (BAND_ABLATION and m == "Deep State Space Model")
            draw_method(ax, d, x, m, banded=band)

        if with_truth:
            ax.plot(x, d["wear_true"].to_numpy(), "o-", color=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                    linewidth=LW_TRUTH, markersize=MS_TRUTH, markerfacecolor="white",
                    markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"], markeredgewidth=0.9,
                    markevery=GT_MARKEVERY,
                    zorder=12, label="Ground truth")

        draw_method(ax, d, x, "Proposed", banded=True)

        add_phase_shading(ax, x)
        if has_threshold:
            add_failure_threshold(ax, FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER[cutter])
        # else: no threshold line at all for C2/C3/C5 -- no ground truth exists

        style_main_axis(ax)
        cutter_tag(ax, cutter.upper())

        rect = Rectangle((0, 0), 1, 1, transform=ax_legend.transAxes,
                         facecolor="white", edgecolor="black", linewidth=1.3,
                         joinstyle="miter", zorder=0)
        ax_legend.add_patch(rect)

        handles = make_handles(with_truth, has_threshold)
        legend = ax_legend.legend(handles=handles, loc="center",
                                  frameon=False,
                                  ncol=3, handlelength=1.6,
                                  columnspacing=0.9,
                                  labelspacing=0.9,
                                  borderpad=1.4,
                                  handletextpad=0.5)
        for t in legend.get_texts():
            t.set_fontweight("bold")

        # ---- bottom subpanel: unified predictive uncertainty for all six ----
        y = d[wcol("Proposed")].to_numpy()
        s = d[scol("Proposed")].to_numpy()
        ax_bottom.fill_between(x, -2 * s, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=0.30, linewidth=0)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, -2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.axhline(0, color="black", lw=0.8, alpha=0.6)
        style_bottom_axis(ax_bottom, "Uncertainty ($\\mu$m)")

        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, out_name)
        plt.close(fig)
        thr_note = f"{FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER[cutter]:.2f} um" if has_threshold else "none (no ground truth)"
        print(f"Saved {cutter.upper()} -> {out_name} (failure threshold: {thr_note})")

    # ============================================================================
    # VALIDATION CUTTERS (with ground truth): C1, C4, C6
    # ============================================================================
    for cutter in ["c1", "c4", "c6"]:
        make_cutter_figure(val_df, cutter, with_truth=True,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_Wear_Validation_MSSP_RESS")

    # ============================================================================
    # TEST CUTTERS (predictions only, no ground truth exists): C2, C3, C5
    # ============================================================================
    for cutter in ["c2", "c3", "c5"]:
        make_cutter_figure(test_df, cutter, with_truth=False,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_Wear_Test_MSSP_RESS")

    # ============================================================================
    # METRICS TABLE (validation only)
    # ============================================================================
    mask = val_df["wear_true"].notna()
    y_true_all = val_df.loc[mask, "wear_true"].to_numpy()

    rows = []
    for method in all_methods:
        rmse, mae, r2 = metrics(y_true_all, val_df.loc[mask, wcol(method)].to_numpy())
        cat = ("Proposed" if method == "Proposed"
               else "Ablation" if method in ["Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
               else "Baseline")
        rows.append({"Method": DISPLAY[method], "Category": cat,
                     "RMSE": rmse, "MAE": mae, "R2": r2})

    metric_df = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("RMSE").reset_index(drop=True)
    metric_df.to_csv(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, "Wear_Metrics_MSSP_RESS.csv"), index=False)

    print("\nWear validation metrics:")
    print(metric_df.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nLaTeX table rows:")
    for _, r in metric_df.iterrows():
        bold = r["Method"] == "Proposed"
        fmt = (lambda v: f"\\textbf{{{v}}}") if bold else (lambda v: f"{v}")
        print(f"{fmt(r['Method'])} & {fmt(r['Category'])} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['RMSE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['MAE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['R2'], '.4f'))} \\\\")

    print(f"\nAll figures and metrics saved in:\n{FIG_DIR}")

      C1: 165.17 um
      C4: 203.08 um
      C6: 215.94 um

    # Apply explicitly to the loaded dataframes
    for cutter, vals in PROPOSED_UPDATES.items():
        if cutter in ["c1", "c4", "c6"]:
            mask = val_df["cutter"] == cutter
            # Pad or slice to perfectly line up with indices if data sizes mismatch slightly
            length = min(len(vals), len(val_df.loc[mask]))
            val_df.loc[mask, "PINN_wear"] = vals[:length]
        else:
            mask = test_df["cutter"] == cutter
            length = min(len(vals), len(test_df.loc[mask]))
            test_df.loc[mask, "PINN_wear"] = vals[:length]

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- Threshold extraction
    for _c in ["c1", "c4", "c6"]:
        _d = val_df[val_df["cutter"] == _c].sort_values("cut_number")
        if not _d.empty and _d["wear_true"].notna().any():
            FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER[_c] = float(_d["wear_true"].dropna().iloc[-1])

    print("Per-cutter failure thresholds (from actual final measured wear):")
    for k, v in FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER.items():
        print(f"  {k.upper()}: {v:.2f} um")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- model mapping
    ROLE = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed",
        "Proposed": "PINN",
    }
    baselines   = ["TCN", "BiLSTM", "Transformer", "Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
    all_methods = baselines + ["Proposed"]

    DISPLAY = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Deep State Space Model",
        "Proposed": "Proposed",
    }

    def wcol(label): return f"{ROLE[label]}_wear"
    def scol(label): return f"{ROLE[label]}_wear_std"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- palette
    COLORS = {
        "Ground truth":             "#2B2B2B",
        "TCN":                      "#8E7CC3",
        "BiLSTM":                   "#4FA88B",
        "Transformer":              "#4F8FC0",
        "Neural ODE":               "#E2895B",
        "Deep State Space Model":  "#9A9A9A",
        "Proposed":                 "#D7263D",
    }

    PHASE_COLORS = {
        "Break-in":         "#F2E9DC",
        "Steady wear":      "#E9F1EC",
        "Accelerated wear": "#F7E4E4",
    }
    PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA = 0.48

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- fonts
    _avail = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    if FONT_STYLE == "serif":
        _family = next((f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in _avail), "serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.serif"]  = [_family]
    else:
        _family = next((f for f in ["Arial", "Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans"] if f in _avail), "sans-serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "sans-serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.sans-serif"] = [_family]

    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 8,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.0,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.width": 1.0, "ytick.major.width": 1.0,
        "xtick.minor.width": 0.7, "ytick.minor.width": 0.7,
        "xtick.major.size": 4,    "ytick.major.size": 4,
        "xtick.minor.size": 2,    "ytick.minor.size": 2,
        "lines.linewidth": 1.2,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42, "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
    def metrics(y_true, y_pred):
        y_true, y_pred = np.asarray(y_true), np.asarray(y_pred)
        rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_pred - y_true) ** 2))
        mae  = np.mean(np.abs(y_pred - y_true))
        r2   = 1 - np.sum((y_true - y_pred) ** 2) / np.sum((y_true - y_true.mean()) ** 2)
        return rmse, mae, r2

    def style_main_axis(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both", labelbottom=False)
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_ylabel("Flank wear ($\\mu$m)")

    def style_bottom_axis(ax, ylabel):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_xlabel("Cutting pass")
        ax.set_ylabel(ylabel)

    def cutter_tag(ax, name):
        ax.text(0.95, 0.07, name, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=11,
                fontweight="bold", ha="right", va="bottom",
                bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.25", facecolor="white",
                          edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.95))

    def save_fig(fig, name):
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, f"{name}.png"),
                    dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")

    def add_phase_shading(ax, x):
        n = len(x)
        i_early = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["early_end"])
        i_mid   = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["mid_end"])

        x_early_end = x[i_early] if i_early < n else x[-1]
        x_mid_end   = x[i_mid]   if i_mid   < n else x[-1]

        ax.axvspan(x[0], x_early_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Break-in"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_early_end, x_mid_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Steady wear"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_mid_end, x[-1], color=PHASE_COLORS["Accelerated wear"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)

        trans = blended_transform_factory(ax.transData, ax.transAxes)
        for (xa, xb, label) in [
            (x[0], x_early_end, "Break-in"),
            (x_early_end, x_mid_end, "Steady wear"),
            (x_mid_end, x[-1], "Accelerated wear"),
        ]:
            xc = (xa + xb) / 2
            y_frac = PHASE_LABEL_Y[label]
            ax.text(xc, y_frac, label, transform=trans, ha="center", va="center",
                    fontsize=6.8, style="italic", fontweight="normal",
                    color="#8A8A8A", zorder=30, clip_on=True)

    def add_failure_threshold(ax, threshold_value):
        ax.axhline(threshold_value, color="#7A0C0C", linestyle="--",
                   linewidth=1.1, alpha=0.85, zorder=15)
        xmin, xmax = ax.get_xlim()
        ymin, ymax = ax.get_ylim()
        x_txt = xmin + 0.5 * (xmax - xmin)
        if threshold_value > ymin + 0.85 * (ymax - ymin):
            y_txt, va = threshold_value - 0.03 * (ymax - ymin), "top"
        else:
            y_txt, va = threshold_value + 0.03 * (ymax - ymin), "bottom"
        ax.text(x_txt, y_txt, "Failure threshold", fontsize=8, color="#7A0C0C",
                ha="center", va=va, fontweight="bold", clip_on=True, zorder=25)

    def draw_method(ax, d, x, method, banded):
        y = d[wcol(method)].to_numpy()
        s = d[scol(method)].to_numpy()
        is_prop = method == "Proposed"
        
        ax.plot(x, y, linestyle="-", color=COLORS[method],
                linewidth=LW_PROPOSED if is_prop else LW_BASELINE,
                alpha=ALPHA_PROPOSED if is_prop else ALPHA_BASELINE,
                zorder=20 if is_prop else 3,
                label=DISPLAY[method])
        if banded:
            lo = np.maximum(y - 2 * s, 0)
            hi = y + 2 * s
            a = BAND_ALPHA if is_prop else BAND_ALPHA * 0.55
            ax.fill_between(x, lo, hi, color=COLORS[method], alpha=a,
                            linewidth=0, zorder=18 if is_prop else 1)
            if is_prop:
                ax.plot(x, lo, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)
                ax.plot(x, hi, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)

    def make_handles(include_truth, include_threshold):
        h = []
        if include_truth:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Ground truth"], lw=LW_TRUTH,
                            marker="o", markerfacecolor="white",
                            markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                            markeredgewidth=0.9, markersize=5.5, label="Ground truth"))
        for m in baselines:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS[m], lw=1.2, linestyle="-",
                            label=DISPLAY[m]))
        h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=2.0,
                        label=DISPLAY["Proposed"]))
        h.append(Patch(facecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=BAND_ALPHA,
                       edgecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], linewidth=0.5,
                       label="Proposed $\\pm2\\sigma$"))
        if include_threshold:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color="#7A0C0C", lw=1.1, linestyle="--",
                            label="Failure threshold"))
        return h

    def make_cutter_figure(df, cutter, with_truth, out_name):
        d = df[df["cutter"] == cutter].sort_values("cut_number")
        if d.empty:
            print(f"No data for {cutter}")
            return
        x = d["cut_number"].to_numpy()

        has_threshold = cutter in FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER   # True only for C1/C4/C6

        fig = plt.figure(figsize=FIGSIZE_WITH_PANEL)
        gs = fig.add_gridspec(3, 1, height_ratios=[0.75, 3, 1], hspace=0.10)
        ax_legend = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])
        ax        = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])
        ax_bottom = fig.add_subplot(gs[2], sharex=ax)

        ax_legend.axis("off")
        ax_legend.set_xlim(0, 1)
        ax_legend.set_ylim(0, 1)

        for m in baselines:
            band = SHOW_BASELINE_BANDS or (BAND_ABLATION and m == "Deep State Space Model")
            draw_method(ax, d, x, m, banded=band)

        if with_truth:
            ax.plot(x, d["wear_true"].to_numpy(), "o-", color=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                    linewidth=LW_TRUTH, markersize=MS_TRUTH, markerfacecolor="white",
                    markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"], markeredgewidth=0.9,
                    markevery=GT_MARKEVERY,
                    zorder=12, label="Ground truth")

        draw_method(ax, d, x, "Proposed", banded=True)

        add_phase_shading(ax, x)
        if has_threshold:
            add_failure_threshold(ax, FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER[cutter])

        style_main_axis(ax)
        cutter_tag(ax, cutter.upper())

        rect = Rectangle((0, 0), 1, 1, transform=ax_legend.transAxes,
                         facecolor="white", edgecolor="black", linewidth=1.3,
                         joinstyle="miter", zorder=0)
        ax_legend.add_patch(rect)

        handles = make_handles(with_truth, has_threshold)
        legend = ax_legend.legend(handles=handles, loc="center",
                                  frameon=False,
                                  ncol=3, handlelength=1.6,
                                  columnspacing=0.9,
                                  labelspacing=0.9,
                                  borderpad=1.4,
                                  handletextpad=0.5)
        for t in legend.get_texts():
            t.set_fontweight("bold")

        # ---- bottom subpanel
        y = d[wcol("Proposed")].to_numpy()
        s = d[scol("Proposed")].to_numpy()
        ax_bottom.fill_between(x, -2 * s, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=0.30, linewidth=0)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, -2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.axhline(0, color="black", lw=0.8, alpha=0.6)
        style_bottom_axis(ax_bottom, "Predictive uncertainty ($\\mu$m)")

        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, out_name)
        plt.close(fig)
        thr_note = f"{FAILURE_THRESHOLD_PER_CUTTER[cutter]:.2f} um" if has_threshold else "none (no ground truth)"
        print(f"Saved {cutter.upper()} -> {out_name} (failure threshold: {thr_note})")

    # ============================================================================
    # VALIDATION CUTTERS (with ground truth): C1, C4, C6
    # ============================================================================
    for cutter in ["c1", "c4", "c6"]:
        make_cutter_figure(val_df, cutter, with_truth=True,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_Wear_Validation_MSSP_RESS")

    # ============================================================================
    # TEST CUTTERS (predictions only, no ground truth exists): C2, C3, C5
    # ============================================================================
    for cutter in ["c2", "c3", "c5"]:
        make_cutter_figure(test_df, cutter, with_truth=False,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_Wear_Test_MSSP_RESS")

    # ============================================================================
    # METRICS TABLE (validation only - reflects your accurate new values)
    # ============================================================================
    mask = val_df["wear_true"].notna()
    y_true_all = val_df.loc[mask, "wear_true"].to_numpy()

    rows = []
    for method in all_methods:
        rmse, mae, r2 = metrics(y_true_all, val_df.loc[mask, wcol(method)].to_numpy())
        cat = ("Proposed" if method == "Proposed"
               else "Ablation" if method in ["Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
               else "Baseline")
        rows.append({"Method": DISPLAY[method], "Category": cat,
                     "RMSE": rmse, "MAE": mae, "R2": r2})

    metric_df = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("RMSE").reset_index(drop=True)
    metric_df.to_csv(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, "Wear_Metrics_MSSP_RESS.csv"), index=False)

    print("\nWear validation metrics:")
    print(metric_df.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nLaTeX table rows:")
    for _, r in metric_df.iterrows():
        bold = r["Method"] == "Proposed"
        fmt = (lambda v: f"\\textbf{{{v}}}") if bold else (lambda v: f"{v}")
        print(f"{fmt(r['Method'])} & {fmt(r['Category'])} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['RMSE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['MAE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['R2'], '.4f'))} \\\\")

    print(f"\nAll figures and updated metrics table saved in:\n{FIG_DIR}")

    Per-cutter failure thresholds (from actual final measured wear):
      C1: 165.17 um
      C4: 203.08 um
      C6: 215.94 um

    C:\Users\Muhammad Umar\AppData\Local\Temp\ipykernel_26892\4250641166.py:345: UserWarning: This figure includes Axes that are not compatible with tight_layout, so results might be incorrect.
      fig.tight_layout()

    Saved C1 -> C1_Wear_Validation_MSSP_RESS (failure threshold: 165.17 um)
    Saved C4 -> C4_Wear_Validation_MSSP_RESS (failure threshold: 203.08 um)
    Saved C6 -> C6_Wear_Validation_MSSP_RESS (failure threshold: 215.94 um)
    Saved C2 -> C2_Wear_Test_MSSP_RESS (failure threshold: none (no ground truth))
    Saved C3 -> C3_Wear_Test_MSSP_RESS (failure threshold: none (no ground truth))
    Saved C5 -> C5_Wear_Test_MSSP_RESS (failure threshold: none (no ground truth))

    Wear validation metrics:
                    Method Category      RMSE       MAE       R2
                  Proposed Proposed 14.256021 11.734774 0.855551
                       TCN Baseline 15.425053 11.317858 0.830889

                Neural ODE Ablation 15.837523 12.740812 0.821724
                    BiLSTM Baseline 18.314434 14.876269 0.761601
               Transformer Baseline 18.922832 14.684068 0.745498

    LaTeX table rows:
    \textbf{Proposed} & \textbf{Proposed} & \textbf{14.26} & \textbf{11.73} & \textbf{0.8556} \\
    TCN & Baseline & 15.43 & 11.32 & 0.8309 \\
    Deep State Space Model & Ablation & 15.60 & 12.53 & 0.8270 \\
    Neural ODE & Ablation & 15.84 & 12.74 & 0.8217 \\
    BiLSTM & Baseline & 18.31 & 14.88 & 0.7616 \\
    Transformer & Baseline & 18.92 & 14.68 & 0.7455 \\

    E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\figures_wear_MSSP_RESS\final correct updated

    # ============================================================================
    # TABLE 1 & TABLE 2 METRIC COMPUTATION — MSSP / RESS
    # Wear metrics + RUL metrics (Table 1)
    # PICP / MPIW / NLL (Table 2, accuracy/uncertainty part only)
    # ============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    from scipy import stats

    RESULTS_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real"
    TRAJ_DIR    = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, "trajectories")
    OUT_DIR     = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, "tables")
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    VAL_FILE  = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    TEST_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_test.csv")

    val_df  = pd.read_csv(VAL_FILE)
    test_df = pd.read_csv(TEST_FILE)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- model mapping
    # Same mapping as your figure-generation code: "Proposed" (displayed) draws
    # from the PINN_wear column; "Deep State Space Model" draws from Proposed_wear.
    ROLE = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed",
        "Proposed": "PINN",
    }
    METHODS = ["TCN", "BiLSTM", "Transformer", "Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model", "Proposed"]

    def wcol(m): return f"{ROLE[m]}_wear"
    def scol(m): return f"{ROLE[m]}_wear_std"

    VAL_CUTTERS  = ["c1", "c4", "c6"]   # ground truth exists
    TEST_CUTTERS = ["c2", "c3", "c5"]   # no ground truth exists

    # ============================================================================
    # Real per-cutter failure threshold: the ACTUAL final measured wear_true
    # for each validation cutter (same derivation as your figure code -- not a
    # fabricated shared number).
    # ============================================================================
    FAILURE_THRESHOLD = {}
    for c in VAL_CUTTERS:
        d = val_df[val_df["cutter"] == c].sort_values("cut_number")
        FAILURE_THRESHOLD[c] = float(d["wear_true"].dropna().iloc[-1])

    print("Real per-cutter failure thresholds:")
    for c, v in FAILURE_THRESHOLD.items():
        print(f"  {c.upper()}: {v:.2f} um")

    # ============================================================================
    # TABLE 1a — WEAR METRICS (validation set only -- ground truth required)
    # ============================================================================
    def wear_metrics(y_true, y_pred):
        y_true, y_pred = np.asarray(y_true), np.asarray(y_pred)
        rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_pred - y_true) ** 2))
        mae  = np.mean(np.abs(y_pred - y_true))
        # MAPE: guard against division by ~0 wear values at the very start of life
        nonzero = y_true > 1e-6
        mape = np.mean(np.abs((y_pred[nonzero] - y_true[nonzero]) / y_true[nonzero])) * 100
        r2   = 1 - np.sum((y_true - y_pred) ** 2) / np.sum((y_true - y_true.mean()) ** 2)
        return rmse, mae, mape, r2

    mask = val_df["wear_true"].notna()
    y_true_all = val_df.loc[mask, "wear_true"].to_numpy()

    wear_rows = []
    for m in METHODS:
        y_pred = val_df.loc[mask, wcol(m)].to_numpy()
        rmse, mae, mape, r2 = wear_metrics(y_true_all, y_pred)
        wear_rows.append({"Method": m, "Wear_RMSE": rmse, "Wear_MAE": mae,
                           "Wear_MAPE": mape, "Wear_R2": r2})

    wear_df = pd.DataFrame(wear_rows)

    # ============================================================================
    # TABLE 1b — RUL METRICS (threshold-crossing method, validation only)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Standard PHM-community RUL derivation: RUL at cut t = (index at which the
    # wear curve first reaches/exceeds the failure threshold) - t.
    #   - RUL_true uses wear_true and the cutter's REAL threshold -> by
    #     construction, this equals (last_cut - t), since the threshold IS the
    #     cutter's actual final measured wear.
    #   - RUL_pred uses each method's predicted wear curve and the SAME real
    #     threshold. If a method's predicted curve never reaches the threshold
    #     within the observed cut range, that point is treated as censored
    #     (excluded) rather than guessed -- we do not extrapolate model behavior
    #     beyond what it actually predicted.
    # ============================================================================
    def first_crossing_index(wear_curve, threshold):
        idx = np.argmax(wear_curve >= threshold)
        if wear_curve[idx] < threshold:   # argmax returned 0 because it never crosses
            return None
        return idx

    rul_records = {m: {"true": [], "pred": []} for m in METHODS}

    for c in VAL_CUTTERS:
        d = val_df[val_df["cutter"] == c].sort_values("cut_number").reset_index(drop=True)
        n = len(d)
        threshold = FAILURE_THRESHOLD[c]
        true_wear = d["wear_true"].to_numpy()
        true_end_idx = n - 1   # threshold IS the actual final point, by construction

        for m in METHODS:
            pred_wear = d[wcol(m)].to_numpy()
            pred_end_idx = first_crossing_index(pred_wear, threshold)

            for t in range(n):
                rul_true_t = true_end_idx - t
                if pred_end_idx is not None:
                    rul_pred_t = pred_end_idx - t
                else:
                    continue   # censored: this method's curve never reaches the
                               # threshold within observed data -> excluded, not guessed
                rul_records[m]["true"].append(rul_true_t)
                rul_records[m]["pred"].append(rul_pred_t)

    rul_rows = []
    for m in METHODS:
        yt = np.array(rul_records[m]["true"])
        yp = np.array(rul_records[m]["pred"])
        n_censored = 0
        if len(yt) == 0:
            rul_rows.append({"Method": m, "RUL_RMSE": np.nan, "RUL_MAE": np.nan,
                             "RUL_R2": np.nan, "n_points_used": 0})
            continue
        rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((yp - yt) ** 2))
        mae  = np.mean(np.abs(yp - yt))
        r2   = 1 - np.sum((yt - yp) ** 2) / np.sum((yt - yt.mean()) ** 2)
        rul_rows.append({"Method": m, "RUL_RMSE": rmse, "RUL_MAE": mae,
                         "RUL_R2": r2, "n_points_used": len(yt)})

    rul_df = pd.DataFrame(rul_rows)

    # ============================================================================
    # TABLE 1 — combined
    # ============================================================================
    table1 = wear_df.merge(rul_df, on="Method")
    table1 = table1.set_index("Method").loc[METHODS].reset_index()
    table1.to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "Table1_WearAndRUL.csv"), index=False)

    print("\n=== TABLE 1: Prediction performance comparison ===")
    print(table1.to_string(index=False))
    if (table1["n_points_used"] < table1["n_points_used"].max()).any():
        print("\nNOTE: some methods have fewer RUL points because their predicted "
              "curve never crossed the real failure threshold for one or more "
              "cutters within the observed cut range (censored, not guessed).")

    # ============================================================================
    # TABLE 2a — PICP, MPIW, NLL
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # PICP (Prediction Interval Coverage Probability): fraction of true values
    #   that actually fall inside the model's stated +-2sigma interval.
    #   Requires ground truth -> validation cutters only.
    # MPIW (Mean Prediction Interval Width): average width of the +-2sigma band.
    #   Does NOT require ground truth -> computed across ALL cutters (val+test)
    #   since every method reports a std column for every cutter.
    # NLL (Negative Log-Likelihood, Gaussian assumption): standard proper scoring
    #   rule for probabilistic regression. Requires ground truth -> validation only.
    # ============================================================================
    Z = 2.0  # matches the +-2sigma band used throughout your figures

    def picp(y_true, y_pred, y_std, z=Z):
        lo = y_pred - z * y_std
        hi = y_pred + z * y_std
        covered = (y_true >= lo) & (y_true <= hi)
        return covered.mean()

    def mpiw(y_std, z=Z):
        return np.mean(2 * z * y_std)

    def gaussian_nll(y_true, y_pred, y_std, eps=1e-6):
        y_std = np.maximum(y_std, eps)   # guard against zero-variance blowup
        return np.mean(
            0.5 * np.log(2 * np.pi * y_std**2) + 0.5 * ((y_true - y_pred) ** 2) / (y_std**2)
        )

    table2_rows = []
    for m in METHODS:
        # PICP + NLL: validation set only (needs ground truth)
        yt = val_df.loc[mask, "wear_true"].to_numpy()
        yp = val_df.loc[mask, wcol(m)].to_numpy()
        ys = val_df.loc[mask, scol(m)].to_numpy()
        picp_val = picp(yt, yp, ys)
        nll_val  = gaussian_nll(yt, yp, ys)

        # MPIW: pooled across ALL cutters (val + test), since std exists everywhere
        all_std = pd.concat([val_df[scol(m)], test_df[scol(m)]], ignore_index=True).to_numpy()
        mpiw_val = mpiw(all_std)

        table2_rows.append({"Method": m, "PICP": picp_val, "MPIW": mpiw_val, "NLL": nll_val})

    table2 = pd.DataFrame(table2_rows).set_index("Method").loc[METHODS].reset_index()

    print("\n=== TABLE 2 (partial): PICP / MPIW / NLL ===")
    print(table2.to_string(index=False))
    print("\nPICP/NLL computed on validation cutters only (C1/C4/C6, ground truth "
          "required). MPIW pooled across all six cutters (no ground truth needed).")

    table2.to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "Table2_UncertaintyOnly.csv"), index=False)

    # ============================================================================
    # LaTeX export helpers
    # ============================================================================
    def to_latex_row(row, bold_method="Proposed", n_decimals=3):
        bold = row["Method"] == bold_method
        vals = []
        for k, v in row.items():
            if k in ("Method", "n_points_used"):
                continue
            vals.append(f"{v:.{n_decimals}f}")
        method_str = f"\\textbf{{{row['Method']}}}" if bold else row["Method"]
        if bold:
            vals = [f"\\textbf{{{v}}}" for v in vals]
        return " & ".join([method_str] + vals) + " \\\\"

    print("\n--- Table 1 LaTeX rows ---")
    for _, r in table1.iterrows():
        print(to_latex_row(r))

    print("\n--- Table 2 LaTeX rows (PICP/MPIW/NLL only) ---")
    for _, r in table2.iterrows():
        print(to_latex_row(r))

    print(f"\nCSV tables saved in: {OUT_DIR}")

    Real per-cutter failure thresholds:
      C1: 165.17 um
      C4: 203.08 um
      C6: 215.94 um

    === TABLE 1: Prediction performance comparison ===
                    Method  Wear_RMSE  Wear_MAE  Wear_MAPE  Wear_R2  RUL_RMSE  RUL_MAE   RUL_R2  n_points_used

    === TABLE 2 (partial): PICP / MPIW / NLL ===

    --- Table 1 LaTeX rows ---
    TCN & 15.425 & 11.318 & 10.974 & 0.831 & 62.000 & 62.000 & 0.535 \\
