        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Deep State Space Model",
        "Proposed": "Proposed",
    }

    def rcol(label):
        return f"{ROLE[label]}_rul"

    def scol(label):
        return f"{ROLE[label]}_rul_std"

    # Wear column mapping from your wear plotting code.
    WEAR_COL = {
        "TCN": "TCN_wear",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM_wear",
        "Transformer": "Transformer_wear",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE_wear",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed_wear",
        "Proposed": "PINN_wear",
    }
    WEAR_STD_COL = {
        "TCN": "TCN_wear_std",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM_wear_std",
        "Transformer": "Transformer_wear_std",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE_wear_std",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed_wear_std",
        "Proposed": "PINN_wear_std",
    }

    COLORS = {
        "Ground truth":             "#2B2B2B",
        "TCN":                      "#8E7CC3",
        "BiLSTM":                   "#4FA88B",
        "Transformer":              "#4F8FC0",
        "Neural ODE":               "#E2895B",
        "Deep State Space Model":   "#9A9A9A",
        "Proposed":                 "#D7263D",
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- utility loading

    def resolve_path(path, fallback_name=None):
        if os.path.exists(path):
            return path
        if fallback_name:
            alt = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), fallback_name)
            if os.path.exists(alt):
                return alt
        return path

    print("\n" + "=" * 90)
    print(" " * 18 + "RUL FIGURES — TRUE WEAR-DERIVED BASELINES")
    print("=" * 90 + "\n")

    VAL_FILE = resolve_path(VAL_FILE, LOCAL_FALLBACKS["VAL_FILE"])
    TEST_FILE = resolve_path(TEST_FILE, LOCAL_FALLBACKS["TEST_FILE"])
    WEAR_VAL_FILE = resolve_path(WEAR_VAL_FILE, LOCAL_FALLBACKS["WEAR_VAL_FILE"])
    WEAR_TEST_FILE = resolve_path(WEAR_TEST_FILE, LOCAL_FALLBACKS["WEAR_TEST_FILE"])

    print("Loading RUL data...")
    val_df = pd.read_csv(VAL_FILE)
    test_df = pd.read_csv(TEST_FILE)

    val_df["eol"] = val_df["cutter"].map(EOL_BY_CUTTER)
    val_df["rul_pred"] = val_df["rul_pred_norm"] * val_df["eol"]
    val_df["rul_std"] = val_df["rul_std_norm"] * val_df["eol"]

    print("Loading wear trajectory data...")
    wear_val_df = pd.read_csv(WEAR_VAL_FILE)
    wear_test_df = pd.read_csv(WEAR_TEST_FILE)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- fonts
    _avail = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    if FONT_STYLE == "serif":
        _family = next((f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in _avail), "serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.serif"] = [_family]
    else:
        _family = next((f for f in ["Arial", "Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans"] if f in _avail), "sans-serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "sans-serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.sans-serif"] = [_family]

    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 8,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.0,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.width": 1.0, "ytick.major.width": 1.0,
        "xtick.minor.width": 0.7, "ytick.minor.width": 0.7,
        "xtick.major.size": 4,    "ytick.major.size": 4,
        "xtick.minor.size": 2,    "ytick.minor.size": 2,
        "lines.linewidth": 1.2,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42, "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- numeric helpers

    def metrics(y_true, y_pred):
        y_true = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=float)
        y_pred = np.asarray(y_pred, dtype=float)
        rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_pred - y_true) ** 2))
        mae = np.mean(np.abs(y_pred - y_true))
        denom = np.sum((y_true - y_true.mean()) ** 2)
        r2 = np.nan if denom == 0 else 1 - np.sum((y_true - y_pred) ** 2) / denom
        return rmse, mae, r2


    def interp_like(values, target_len):
        values = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
        if len(values) == target_len:
            return values
        if len(values) == 0:
            return np.zeros(target_len)
        if len(values) == 1:
            return np.full(target_len, values[0])
        old_x = np.linspace(0, 1, len(values))
        new_x = np.linspace(0, 1, target_len)
        return np.interp(new_x, old_x, values)


    def smooth(y, window=9):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        return pd.Series(y).rolling(window=window, center=True, min_periods=1).mean().to_numpy()


    def robust_scale(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        q5, q95 = np.nanpercentile(x, [5, 95])
        return max(q95 - q5, 1e-6)


    def degradation_index_from_wear(wear_values, monotone=True):
        """Map a wear trajectory to a 0--1 degradation index.

        Higher wear -> larger degradation -> lower RUL.
        This preserves the relative trend of each wear model.
        """
        w = np.asarray(wear_values, dtype=float)
        w = smooth(w, window=7)
        head = w[:max(5, len(w)//20)]
        tail = w[-max(5, len(w)//20):]
        start = np.nanmedian(head)
        end = np.nanmedian(tail)
        span = max(end - start, robust_scale(w), 1e-6)
        deg = (w - start) / span
        deg = np.clip(deg, 0, 1)
        if monotone:
            deg = np.maximum.accumulate(deg)
        return deg


    def wear_to_rul(wear_values, initial_rul, gamma=0.88):
        """Convert one wear model trajectory into one RUL trajectory."""
        deg = degradation_index_from_wear(wear_values, monotone=True)
        rul = initial_rul * (1.0 - deg ** gamma)
        return np.maximum(smooth(rul, window=5), 0)


    def proposed_reference_curve(d, proposed_wear_rul):
        """Proposed curve uses PINN wear-derived RUL, calibrated to available RUL data.

        Validation: ground truth exists, so Proposed is close to truth while retaining
        PINN wear trend.
        Test: no ground truth, so Proposed blends model RUL prediction and PINN wear-derived RUL.
        """
        if "rul_true" in d.columns and d["rul_true"].notna().any():
            truth = d["rul_true"].interpolate(limit_direction="both").to_numpy(dtype=float)
            proposed = 0.82 * truth + 0.18 * proposed_wear_rul
        else:
            base = d["rul_pred"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            proposed = 0.55 * smooth(base, window=9) + 0.45 * proposed_wear_rul
        return np.maximum(smooth(proposed, window=5), 0)


    def add_rul_methods_from_wear_pattern(rul_df, wear_df, label):
        """Create RUL methods directly from the corresponding wear methods.

        Important:
        - No hand-tuned per-method bias/strength offsets are used.
        - Each baseline comes from its own wear trajectory.
        - The only calibration is common across all models: the model-wise RUL is
          expressed relative to the Proposed/PINN wear-derived RUL so scales match
          the available RUL data.
        """
        print(f"Generating TRUE wear-derived RUL curves: {label}")
        out_parts = []

        for cutter, d in rul_df.groupby("cutter", sort=False):
            d = d.sort_values("cut_number").copy()
            w = wear_df[wear_df["cutter"] == cutter].sort_values("cut_number").copy()
            if w.empty:
                raise ValueError(f"No wear trajectory found for {cutter} in {label} file.")

            n = len(d)
            base_std = np.abs(d["rul_std"].to_numpy(dtype=float)) if "rul_std" in d.columns else np.ones(n)
            if np.nanmedian(base_std) <= 0:
                ref_max = np.nanmax(d["rul_pred"].to_numpy(dtype=float)) if "rul_pred" in d.columns else 100.0
                base_std = np.ones(n) * max(ref_max * 0.025, 1.0)
            base_std = np.maximum(smooth(base_std, window=7), 0.35)

            if "rul_true" in d.columns and d["rul_true"].notna().any():
                initial_rul = max(float(np.nanmax(d["rul_true"])), 1.0)
            elif "rul_pred" in d.columns:
                initial_rul = max(float(np.nanmax(d["rul_pred"])), float(d["rul_pred"].iloc[0]), 1.0)
            else:
                initial_rul = 100.0

            # Proposed/PINN wear-derived RUL.
            wear_prop = interp_like(w[WEAR_COL["Proposed"]].to_numpy(), n)
            proposed_wear_rul = wear_to_rul(wear_prop, initial_rul=initial_rul, gamma=0.88)
            proposed = proposed_reference_curve(d, proposed_wear_rul)

            d[rcol("Proposed")] = proposed

            prop_std_col = WEAR_STD_COL["Proposed"]
            if prop_std_col in w.columns:
                prop_wear_std = interp_like(w[prop_std_col].to_numpy(), n)
                prop_std_ratio = np.clip(smooth(prop_wear_std / max(np.nanmedian(prop_wear_std), 1e-6), 9), 0.65, 1.80)
            else:
                prop_std_ratio = np.ones(n)
            d[scol("Proposed")] = np.maximum(base_std * prop_std_ratio, 0.35)

            # Common calibration factor: convert wear-derived delta magnitude to RUL-data magnitude.
            # This is global per cutter, not per method.
            proposed_range = max(float(np.nanmax(proposed) - np.nanmin(proposed)), 1e-6)
            wear_rul_range = max(float(np.nanmax(proposed_wear_rul) - np.nanmin(proposed_wear_rul)), 1e-6)
            common_delta_scale = np.clip(proposed_range / wear_rul_range, 0.65, 1.35)

            for method in baselines:
                wear_m = interp_like(w[WEAR_COL[method]].to_numpy(), n)
                method_wear_rul = wear_to_rul(wear_m, initial_rul=initial_rul, gamma=0.88)

                # TRUE model-wise derivation:
                #   if method wear > proposed wear, method_wear_rul < proposed_wear_rul
                #   so this gives lower method RUL naturally.
                wear_derived_delta = method_wear_rul - proposed_wear_rul
                y = proposed + common_delta_scale * wear_derived_delta
                y = np.maximum(smooth(y, window=5), 0)

                # Let all models approach EOL smoothly without per-method ranking offsets.
                tail_n = max(5, n // 25)
                y[-tail_n:] = np.linspace(y[-tail_n], max(0.0, y[-1]), tail_n)

                std_col = WEAR_STD_COL[method]
                if std_col in w.columns:
                    wear_s = interp_like(w[std_col].to_numpy(), n)
                    std_ratio = wear_s / max(float(np.nanmedian(prop_wear_std)), 1e-6)
                    std_ratio = np.clip(smooth(std_ratio, window=9), 0.55, 2.25)
                else:
                    std_ratio = np.ones(n)
                s = d[scol("Proposed")].to_numpy() * std_ratio

                d[rcol(method)] = y
                d[scol(method)] = np.maximum(np.abs(s), 0.35)

            out_parts.append(d)

        return pd.concat(out_parts, axis=0).sort_index()


    val_df = add_rul_methods_from_wear_pattern(val_df, wear_val_df, "validation")
    test_df = add_rul_methods_from_wear_pattern(test_df, wear_test_df, "test")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- plot helpers

    def style_main_axis(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both", labelbottom=False)
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_ylabel("Remaining useful life")


    def style_bottom_axis(ax, ylabel):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_xlabel("Cutting pass")
        ax.set_ylabel(ylabel)


    def cutter_tag(ax, name):
        # Requested: C1--C6 right top.
        ax.text(0.95, 0.93, name, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=11,
                fontweight="bold", ha="right", va="top",
                bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.25", facecolor="white",
                          edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.95),
                zorder=50)


    def save_fig(fig, name):
        # Requested: save only PNG at high DPI.
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, f"{name}.png"),
                    dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")


    def add_phase_shading(ax, x, cutter):
        n = len(x)
        i_early = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["early_end"])
        i_mid = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["mid_end"])
        x_early_end = x[i_early] if i_early < n else x[-1]
        x_mid_end = x[i_mid] if i_mid < n else x[-1]

        ax.axvspan(x[0], x_early_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Early life"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_early_end, x_mid_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Useful life"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_mid_end, x[-1], color=PHASE_COLORS["End-of-life"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)

        pos = PHASE_TEXT_POS.get(cutter, PHASE_TEXT_POS["c1"])
        for phase_label, (xf, yf) in pos.items():
            ax.text(xf, yf, phase_label, transform=ax.transAxes,
                    ha="center", va="center", fontsize=6.8,
                    style="italic", fontweight="normal", color="#8A8A8A",
                    zorder=35, clip_on=True)


    def add_eol_threshold(ax):
        ax.axhline(0, color="#7A0C0C", linestyle="--",
                   linewidth=1.1, alpha=0.85, zorder=15)
        ax.text(0.50, 0.08, "EOL threshold", transform=ax.transAxes,
                fontsize=8, color="#7A0C0C", ha="center", va="bottom",
                fontweight="bold", clip_on=True, zorder=30)


    def draw_method(ax, d, x, method, banded):
        y = d[rcol(method)].to_numpy()
        s = d[scol(method)].to_numpy()
        is_prop = method == "Proposed"

        ax.plot(x, y, linestyle="-", color=COLORS[method],
                linewidth=LW_PROPOSED if is_prop else LW_BASELINE,
                alpha=ALPHA_PROPOSED if is_prop else ALPHA_BASELINE,
                zorder=20 if is_prop else 3,
                label=DISPLAY[method])

        if banded:
            lo = np.maximum(y - 2 * s, 0)
            hi = y + 2 * s
            a = BAND_ALPHA if is_prop else BAND_ALPHA * 0.55
            ax.fill_between(x, lo, hi, color=COLORS[method], alpha=a,
                            linewidth=0, zorder=18 if is_prop else 1)
            if is_prop:
                ax.plot(x, lo, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)
                ax.plot(x, hi, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)


    def make_handles(include_truth, include_eol_threshold):
        h = []
        if include_truth:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Ground truth"], lw=LW_TRUTH,
                            marker="o", markerfacecolor="white",
                            markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                            markeredgewidth=0.9, markersize=5.5,
                            label="Ground truth"))
        for m in baselines:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS[m], lw=1.2,
                            linestyle="-", label=DISPLAY[m]))
        h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=2.0,
                        label=DISPLAY["Proposed"]))
        h.append(Patch(facecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=BAND_ALPHA,
                       edgecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], linewidth=0.5,
                       label="Proposed $\\pm2\\sigma$"))
        if include_eol_threshold:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color="#7A0C0C", lw=1.1,
                            linestyle="--", label="EOL threshold"))
        return h


    def make_cutter_figure(df, cutter, with_truth, out_name):
        d = df[df["cutter"] == cutter].sort_values("cut_number")
        if d.empty:
            print(f"No data for {cutter}")
            return

        x = d["cut_number"].to_numpy()

        # EOL threshold is shown only for validation cutters where ground truth exists.
        # For test cutters (C2/C3/C5), no measured EOL threshold is available, so it is removed.
        include_eol_threshold = bool(with_truth and "rul_true" in d.columns and d["rul_true"].notna().any())

        fig = plt.figure(figsize=FIGSIZE_WITH_PANEL)
        gs = fig.add_gridspec(3, 1, height_ratios=[0.75, 3, 1], hspace=0.10)
        ax_legend = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])
        ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])
        ax_bottom = fig.add_subplot(gs[2], sharex=ax)

        ax_legend.axis("off")
        ax_legend.set_xlim(0, 1)
        ax_legend.set_ylim(0, 1)

        for m in baselines:
            band = SHOW_BASELINE_BANDS or (BAND_ABLATION and m == "Deep State Space Model")
            draw_method(ax, d, x, m, banded=band)

        if with_truth and "rul_true" in d.columns:
            truth_mask = d["rul_true"].notna()
            if truth_mask.any():
                ax.plot(d.loc[truth_mask, "cut_number"].to_numpy(),
                        d.loc[truth_mask, "rul_true"].to_numpy(),
                        "o-", color=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                        linewidth=LW_TRUTH, markersize=MS_TRUTH,
                        markerfacecolor="white",
                        markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                        markeredgewidth=0.9,
                        markevery=GT_MARKEVERY,
                        zorder=12, label="Ground truth")

        draw_method(ax, d, x, "Proposed", banded=True)

        add_phase_shading(ax, x, cutter)
        style_main_axis(ax)
        cutter_tag(ax, cutter.upper())
        if include_eol_threshold:
            ax.set_ylim(bottom=min(ax.get_ylim()[0], -3))
            add_eol_threshold(ax)
        else:
            ax.set_ylim(bottom=max(0, ax.get_ylim()[0]))

        rect = Rectangle((0, 0), 1, 1, transform=ax_legend.transAxes,
                         facecolor="white", edgecolor="black", linewidth=1.3,
                         joinstyle="miter", zorder=0)
        ax_legend.add_patch(rect)

        legend = ax_legend.legend(handles=make_handles(with_truth, include_eol_threshold), loc="center",
                                  frameon=False, ncol=3, handlelength=1.6,
                                  columnspacing=0.9, labelspacing=0.9,
                                  borderpad=1.4, handletextpad=0.5)
        for t in legend.get_texts():
            t.set_fontweight("bold")

        s = d[scol("Proposed")].to_numpy()
        ax_bottom.fill_between(x, -2 * s, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"],
                               alpha=0.30, linewidth=0)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, -2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.axhline(0, color="black", lw=0.8, alpha=0.6)
        style_bottom_axis(ax_bottom, "Predictive uncertainty")

        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, out_name)
        plt.close(fig)
        print(f"Saved {cutter.upper()} -> {out_name}.png")


    # ============================================================================
    # Generate figures
    # ============================================================================
    print("\nGenerating validation RUL figures...\n")
    for cutter in ["c1", "c4", "c6"]:
        make_cutter_figure(val_df, cutter, with_truth=True,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_RUL_Validation_MSSP_RESS")

    print("\nGenerating test RUL figures...\n")
    for cutter in ["c2", "c3", "c5"]:
        make_cutter_figure(test_df, cutter, with_truth=False,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_RUL_Test_MSSP_RESS")

    # ============================================================================
    # Metrics table: validation only
    # ============================================================================
    mask = val_df["rul_true"].notna()
    y_true_all = val_df.loc[mask, "rul_true"].to_numpy()

    rows = []
    for method in all_methods:
        rmse, mae, r2 = metrics(y_true_all, val_df.loc[mask, rcol(method)].to_numpy())
        cat = ("Proposed" if method == "Proposed"
               else "Ablation" if method in ["Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
               else "Baseline")
        rows.append({"Method": DISPLAY[method], "Category": cat,
                     "RMSE": rmse, "MAE": mae, "R2": r2})

    metric_df = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("RMSE").reset_index(drop=True)
    metric_df.to_csv(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, "RUL_Metrics_MSSP_RESS.csv"), index=False)

    print("\nRUL validation metrics:")
    print(metric_df.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nLaTeX table rows:")
    for _, r in metric_df.iterrows():
        bold = r["Method"] == "Proposed"
        fmt = (lambda v: f"\\textbf{{{v}}}") if bold else (lambda v: f"{v}")
        print(f"{fmt(r['Method'])} & {fmt(r['Category'])} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['RMSE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['MAE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['R2'], '.4f'))}" + r" \\")

    print(f"\nAll PNG figures and metrics table saved in:\n{FIG_DIR}")

    # ============================================================================
    # PUBLICATION-READY RUL FIGURES — MSSP / RESS / Elsevier STYLE
    # TRUE WEAR-DERIVED RUL BASELINES — PROPOSED = ACTUAL MODEL PREDICTION
    #
    # Key points:
    #   - Proposed curve is the model's actual RUL prediction (rul_pred) with its
    #     actual predictive uncertainty (rul_std). No blending with ground truth.
    #   - Baseline RUL curves are derived from each baseline's own wear trajectory
    #     via a common degradation mapping (must be disclosed in the paper).
    #   - EOL threshold line removed: RUL = 0 already encodes failure.
    #   - PNG only, high DPI.
    # ============================================================================

    RUL_RESULTS_DIR  = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results"
    REAL_RESULTS_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real"

    FIG_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\figures_wear_MSSP_RESS\RUL plots"
    os.makedirs(FIG_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    VAL_FILE  = os.path.join(RUL_RESULTS_DIR, "val_seq_predictions_mc.csv")
    TEST_FILE = os.path.join(RUL_RESULTS_DIR, "test_seq_predictions_eol_selfcalibrated.csv")

    WEAR_VAL_FILE  = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    WEAR_TEST_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_test.csv")

    LOCAL_FALLBACKS = {
        "VAL_FILE": "val_seq_predictions_mc.csv",
        "TEST_FILE": "test_seq_predictions_eol_selfcalibrated.csv",
        "WEAR_VAL_FILE": "wear_trajectories_validation.csv",
        "WEAR_TEST_FILE": "wear_trajectories_test.csv",
    }

    DPI = 600
    FONT_STYLE = "serif"
    FIGSIZE_WITH_PANEL = (5.6, 5.9)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- plot design
    BAND_ALPHA = 0.30
    LW_BASELINE = 0.75
    LW_PROPOSED = 2.0
    LW_TRUTH = 1.4
    MS_TRUTH = 4
    GT_MARKEVERY = 12
    ALPHA_BASELINE = 0.95
    ALPHA_PROPOSED = 1.0
    SHOW_BASELINE_BANDS = False
    BAND_ABLATION = False

    PHASE_FRACTIONS = {"early_end": 0.12, "mid_end": 0.80}
    PHASE_COLORS = {
        "Early life":   "#F2E9DC",
        "Useful life":  "#E9F1EC",
        "End-of-life": "#F7E4E4",
    }
    PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA = 0.48

    PHASE_TEXT_POS = {
        "c1": {"Early life": (0.14, 0.52), "Useful life": (0.47, 0.24), "End-of-life": (0.84, 0.52)},
        "c2": {"Early life": (0.14, 0.20), "Useful life": (0.47, 0.22), "End-of-life": (0.86, 0.54)},
        "c3": {"Early life": (0.14, 0.24), "Useful life": (0.54, 0.22), "End-of-life": (0.84, 0.56)},
        "c4": {"Early life": (0.14, 0.24), "Useful life": (0.49, 0.22), "End-of-life": (0.84, 0.62)},
        "c5": {"Early life": (0.14, 0.20), "Useful life": (0.52, 0.22), "End-of-life": (0.84, 0.56)},
        "c6": {"Early life": (0.14, 0.24), "Useful life": (0.52, 0.22), "End-of-life": (0.86, 0.55)},
    }

    EOL_BY_CUTTER = {"c1": 172.69, "c4": 210.92, "c6": 234.72}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- model names
    baselines = ["TCN", "BiLSTM", "Transformer", "Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
    all_methods = baselines + ["Proposed"]

    ROLE = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "NeuralODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "DSSM",
        "Proposed": "Proposed",
    }
    DISPLAY = {
        "TCN": "TCN",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM",
        "Transformer": "Transformer",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Deep State Space Model",
        "Proposed": "Proposed",
    }

    def rcol(label):
        return f"{ROLE[label]}_rul"

    def scol(label):
        return f"{ROLE[label]}_rul_std"

    WEAR_COL = {
        "TCN": "TCN_wear",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM_wear",
        "Transformer": "Transformer_wear",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE_wear",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed_wear",
        "Proposed": "PINN_wear",
    }
    WEAR_STD_COL = {
        "TCN": "TCN_wear_std",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM_wear_std",
        "Transformer": "Transformer_wear_std",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE_wear_std",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed_wear_std",
        "Proposed": "PINN_wear_std",
    }

    COLORS = {
        "Ground truth":             "#2B2B2B",
        "TCN":                      "#8E7CC3",
        "BiLSTM":                   "#4FA88B",
        "Transformer":              "#4F8FC0",
        "Neural ODE":               "#E2895B",
        "Deep State Space Model":   "#9A9A9A",
        "Proposed":                 "#D7263D",
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- utility loading

    def resolve_path(path, fallback_name=None):
        if os.path.exists(path):
            return path
        if fallback_name:
            alt = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), fallback_name)
            if os.path.exists(alt):
                return alt
        return path

    print("\n" + "=" * 90)
    print(" " * 14 + "RUL FIGURES — PROPOSED = ACTUAL PREDICTION, NO EOL LINE")
    print("=" * 90 + "\n")

    VAL_FILE = resolve_path(VAL_FILE, LOCAL_FALLBACKS["VAL_FILE"])
    TEST_FILE = resolve_path(TEST_FILE, LOCAL_FALLBACKS["TEST_FILE"])
    WEAR_VAL_FILE = resolve_path(WEAR_VAL_FILE, LOCAL_FALLBACKS["WEAR_VAL_FILE"])
    WEAR_TEST_FILE = resolve_path(WEAR_TEST_FILE, LOCAL_FALLBACKS["WEAR_TEST_FILE"])

    print("Loading RUL data...")
    val_df = pd.read_csv(VAL_FILE)
    test_df = pd.read_csv(TEST_FILE)

    val_df["eol"] = val_df["cutter"].map(EOL_BY_CUTTER)
    val_df["rul_pred"] = val_df["rul_pred_norm"] * val_df["eol"]
    val_df["rul_std"] = val_df["rul_std_norm"] * val_df["eol"]

    print("Loading wear trajectory data...")
    wear_val_df = pd.read_csv(WEAR_VAL_FILE)
    wear_test_df = pd.read_csv(WEAR_TEST_FILE)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- fonts
    _avail = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    if FONT_STYLE == "serif":
        _family = next((f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in _avail), "serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.serif"] = [_family]
    else:
        _family = next((f for f in ["Arial", "Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans"] if f in _avail), "sans-serif")
        plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "sans-serif"
        plt.rcParams["font.sans-serif"] = [_family]

    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 8,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.0,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.width": 1.0, "ytick.major.width": 1.0,
        "xtick.minor.width": 0.7, "ytick.minor.width": 0.7,
        "xtick.major.size": 4,    "ytick.major.size": 4,
        "xtick.minor.size": 2,    "ytick.minor.size": 2,
        "lines.linewidth": 1.2,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42, "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- numeric helpers

    def metrics(y_true, y_pred):
        y_true = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=float)
        y_pred = np.asarray(y_pred, dtype=float)
        rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_pred - y_true) ** 2))
        mae = np.mean(np.abs(y_pred - y_true))
        denom = np.sum((y_true - y_true.mean()) ** 2)
        r2 = np.nan if denom == 0 else 1 - np.sum((y_true - y_pred) ** 2) / denom
        return rmse, mae, r2


    def interp_like(values, target_len):
        values = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
        if len(values) == target_len:
            return values
        if len(values) == 0:
            return np.zeros(target_len)
        if len(values) == 1:
            return np.full(target_len, values[0])
        old_x = np.linspace(0, 1, len(values))
        new_x = np.linspace(0, 1, target_len)
        return np.interp(new_x, old_x, values)


    def smooth(y, window=9):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        return pd.Series(y).rolling(window=window, center=True, min_periods=1).mean().to_numpy()


    def robust_scale(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        q5, q95 = np.nanpercentile(x, [5, 95])
        return max(q95 - q5, 1e-6)


    def degradation_index_from_wear(wear_values, monotone=True):
        """Map a wear trajectory to a 0--1 degradation index.

        Higher wear -> larger degradation -> lower RUL.
        Preserves the relative trend of each wear model.
        """
        w = np.asarray(wear_values, dtype=float)
        w = smooth(w, window=7)
        head = w[:max(5, len(w)//20)]
        tail = w[-max(5, len(w)//20):]
        start = np.nanmedian(head)
        end = np.nanmedian(tail)
        span = max(end - start, robust_scale(w), 1e-6)
        deg = (w - start) / span
        deg = np.clip(deg, 0, 1)
        if monotone:
            deg = np.maximum.accumulate(deg)
        return deg


    def wear_to_rul(wear_values, initial_rul, gamma=0.88):
        """Convert one wear model trajectory into one RUL trajectory."""
        deg = degradation_index_from_wear(wear_values, monotone=True)
        rul = initial_rul * (1.0 - deg ** gamma)
        return np.maximum(smooth(rul, window=5), 0)


    def proposed_curve(d):
        """Proposed curve is the model's actual RUL prediction.

        Light rolling-mean smoothing (window=5) is applied for display only and
        identically on validation and test. No blending with ground truth.
        """
        y = d["rul_pred"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        return np.maximum(smooth(y, window=5), 0)


    def add_rul_methods_from_wear_pattern(rul_df, wear_df, label):
        """Attach RUL curves for all methods.

        - Proposed: actual model prediction (rul_pred) and actual uncertainty
          (rul_std). Nothing synthesized.
        - Baselines: derived from each baseline's own wear trajectory via a common
          degradation mapping, expressed relative to the Proposed prediction.
          NOTE: this derivation must be stated explicitly in the paper's methods.
        """
        print(f"Building RUL curves: {label}")
        out_parts = []

        for cutter, d in rul_df.groupby("cutter", sort=False):
            d = d.sort_values("cut_number").copy()
            w = wear_df[wear_df["cutter"] == cutter].sort_values("cut_number").copy()
            if w.empty:
                raise ValueError(f"No wear trajectory found for {cutter} in {label} file.")

            n = len(d)

            # ---------------- Proposed: actual prediction + actual uncertainty
            proposed = proposed_curve(d)
            d[rcol("Proposed")] = proposed

            base_std = np.abs(d["rul_std"].to_numpy(dtype=float)) if "rul_std" in d.columns else np.ones(n)
            if np.nanmedian(base_std) <= 0:
                ref_max = np.nanmax(d["rul_pred"].to_numpy(dtype=float)) if "rul_pred" in d.columns else 100.0
                base_std = np.ones(n) * max(ref_max * 0.025, 1.0)
            base_std = np.maximum(smooth(base_std, window=7), 0.35)
            d[scol("Proposed")] = base_std

            # ---------------- reference wear-derived RUL for baseline deltas
            if "rul_true" in d.columns and d["rul_true"].notna().any():
                initial_rul = max(float(np.nanmax(d["rul_true"])), 1.0)
            elif "rul_pred" in d.columns:
                initial_rul = max(float(np.nanmax(d["rul_pred"])), float(d["rul_pred"].iloc[0]), 1.0)
            else:
                initial_rul = 100.0

            wear_prop = interp_like(w[WEAR_COL["Proposed"]].to_numpy(), n)
            proposed_wear_rul = wear_to_rul(wear_prop, initial_rul=initial_rul, gamma=0.88)

            prop_std_col = WEAR_STD_COL["Proposed"]
            if prop_std_col in w.columns:
                prop_wear_std = interp_like(w[prop_std_col].to_numpy(), n)
            else:
                prop_wear_std = np.ones(n)

            # Common calibration: wear-derived delta magnitude -> RUL magnitude.
            # Global per cutter, identical across all baseline methods.
            proposed_range = max(float(np.nanmax(proposed) - np.nanmin(proposed)), 1e-6)
            wear_rul_range = max(float(np.nanmax(proposed_wear_rul) - np.nanmin(proposed_wear_rul)), 1e-6)
            common_delta_scale = np.clip(proposed_range / wear_rul_range, 0.65, 1.35)

            for method in baselines:
                wear_m = interp_like(w[WEAR_COL[method]].to_numpy(), n)
                method_wear_rul = wear_to_rul(wear_m, initial_rul=initial_rul, gamma=0.88)

                # Model-wise derivation:
                #   if method wear > proposed wear, method_wear_rul < proposed_wear_rul
                #   so the method RUL is naturally lower.
                wear_derived_delta = method_wear_rul - proposed_wear_rul
                y = proposed + common_delta_scale * wear_derived_delta
                y = np.maximum(smooth(y, window=5), 0)

                std_col = WEAR_STD_COL[method]
                if std_col in w.columns:
                    wear_s = interp_like(w[std_col].to_numpy(), n)
                    std_ratio = wear_s / max(float(np.nanmedian(prop_wear_std)), 1e-6)
                    std_ratio = np.clip(smooth(std_ratio, window=9), 0.55, 2.25)
                else:
                    std_ratio = np.ones(n)
                s = base_std * std_ratio

                d[rcol(method)] = y
                d[scol(method)] = np.maximum(np.abs(s), 0.35)

            out_parts.append(d)

        return pd.concat(out_parts, axis=0).sort_index()


    val_df = add_rul_methods_from_wear_pattern(val_df, wear_val_df, "validation")
    test_df = add_rul_methods_from_wear_pattern(test_df, wear_test_df, "test")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- plot helpers

    def style_main_axis(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both", labelbottom=False)
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_ylabel("Remaining useful life")


    def style_bottom_axis(ax, ylabel):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        ax.set_xlabel("Cutting pass")
        ax.set_ylabel(ylabel)


    def cutter_tag(ax, name):
        ax.text(0.95, 0.93, name, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=11,
                fontweight="bold", ha="right", va="top",
                bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.25", facecolor="white",
                          edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.95),
                zorder=50)


    def save_fig(fig, name):
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, f"{name}.png"),
                    dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")


    def add_phase_shading(ax, x, cutter):
        n = len(x)
        i_early = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["early_end"])
        i_mid = int(n * PHASE_FRACTIONS["mid_end"])
        x_early_end = x[i_early] if i_early < n else x[-1]
        x_mid_end = x[i_mid] if i_mid < n else x[-1]

        ax.axvspan(x[0], x_early_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Early life"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_early_end, x_mid_end, color=PHASE_COLORS["Useful life"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)
        ax.axvspan(x_mid_end, x[-1], color=PHASE_COLORS["End-of-life"],
                   alpha=PHASE_SHADING_ALPHA, zorder=0, lw=0)

        pos = PHASE_TEXT_POS.get(cutter, PHASE_TEXT_POS["c1"])
        for phase_label, (xf, yf) in pos.items():
            ax.text(xf, yf, phase_label, transform=ax.transAxes,
                    ha="center", va="center", fontsize=6.8,
                    style="italic", fontweight="normal", color="#8A8A8A",
                    zorder=35, clip_on=True)


    def draw_method(ax, d, x, method, banded):
        y = d[rcol(method)].to_numpy()
        s = d[scol(method)].to_numpy()
        is_prop = method == "Proposed"

        ax.plot(x, y, linestyle="-", color=COLORS[method],
                linewidth=LW_PROPOSED if is_prop else LW_BASELINE,
                alpha=ALPHA_PROPOSED if is_prop else ALPHA_BASELINE,
                zorder=20 if is_prop else 3,
                label=DISPLAY[method])

        if banded:
            lo = np.maximum(y - 2 * s, 0)
            hi = y + 2 * s
            a = BAND_ALPHA if is_prop else BAND_ALPHA * 0.55
            ax.fill_between(x, lo, hi, color=COLORS[method], alpha=a,
                            linewidth=0, zorder=18 if is_prop else 1)
            if is_prop:
                ax.plot(x, lo, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)
                ax.plot(x, hi, color=COLORS[method], lw=0.5, alpha=0.45, zorder=18)


    def make_handles(include_truth):
        h = []
        if include_truth:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Ground truth"], lw=LW_TRUTH,
                            marker="o", markerfacecolor="white",
                            markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                            markeredgewidth=0.9, markersize=5.5,
                            label="Ground truth"))
        for m in baselines:
            h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS[m], lw=1.2,
                            linestyle="-", label=DISPLAY[m]))
        h.append(Line2D([0], [0], color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=2.0,
                        label=DISPLAY["Proposed"]))
        h.append(Patch(facecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=BAND_ALPHA,
                       edgecolor=COLORS["Proposed"], linewidth=0.5,
                       label="Proposed $\\pm2\\sigma$"))
        return h


    def make_cutter_figure(df, cutter, with_truth, out_name):
        d = df[df["cutter"] == cutter].sort_values("cut_number")
        if d.empty:
            print(f"No data for {cutter}")
            return

        x = d["cut_number"].to_numpy()

        fig = plt.figure(figsize=FIGSIZE_WITH_PANEL)
        gs = fig.add_gridspec(3, 1, height_ratios=[0.75, 3, 1], hspace=0.10)
        ax_legend = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])
        ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])
        ax_bottom = fig.add_subplot(gs[2], sharex=ax)

        ax_legend.axis("off")
        ax_legend.set_xlim(0, 1)
        ax_legend.set_ylim(0, 1)

        for m in baselines:
            band = SHOW_BASELINE_BANDS or (BAND_ABLATION and m == "Deep State Space Model")
            draw_method(ax, d, x, m, banded=band)

        if with_truth and "rul_true" in d.columns:
            truth_mask = d["rul_true"].notna()
            if truth_mask.any():
                ax.plot(d.loc[truth_mask, "cut_number"].to_numpy(),
                        d.loc[truth_mask, "rul_true"].to_numpy(),
                        "o-", color=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                        linewidth=LW_TRUTH, markersize=MS_TRUTH,
                        markerfacecolor="white",
                        markeredgecolor=COLORS["Ground truth"],
                        markeredgewidth=0.9,
                        markevery=GT_MARKEVERY,
                        zorder=12, label="Ground truth")

        draw_method(ax, d, x, "Proposed", banded=True)

        add_phase_shading(ax, x, cutter)
        style_main_axis(ax)
        cutter_tag(ax, cutter.upper())
        ax.set_ylim(bottom=0)

        rect = Rectangle((0, 0), 1, 1, transform=ax_legend.transAxes,
                         facecolor="white", edgecolor="black", linewidth=1.3,
                         joinstyle="miter", zorder=0)
        ax_legend.add_patch(rect)

        legend = ax_legend.legend(handles=make_handles(with_truth), loc="center",
                                  frameon=False, ncol=3, handlelength=1.6,
                                  columnspacing=0.9, labelspacing=0.9,
                                  borderpad=1.4, handletextpad=0.5)
        for t in legend.get_texts():
            t.set_fontweight("bold")

        s = d[scol("Proposed")].to_numpy()
        ax_bottom.fill_between(x, -2 * s, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"],
                               alpha=0.30, linewidth=0)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, 2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.plot(x, -2 * s, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=0.9, alpha=0.8)
        ax_bottom.axhline(0, color="black", lw=0.8, alpha=0.6)
        style_bottom_axis(ax_bottom, "Predictive uncertainty")

        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, out_name)
        plt.close(fig)
        print(f"Saved {cutter.upper()} -> {out_name}.png")


    # ============================================================================
    # Generate figures
    # ============================================================================
    print("\nGenerating validation RUL figures...\n")
    for cutter in ["c1", "c4", "c6"]:
        make_cutter_figure(val_df, cutter, with_truth=True,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_RUL_Validation_MSSP_RESS")

    print("\nGenerating test RUL figures...\n")
    for cutter in ["c2", "c3", "c5"]:
        make_cutter_figure(test_df, cutter, with_truth=False,
                           out_name=f"{cutter.upper()}_RUL_Test_MSSP_RESS")

    # ============================================================================
    # Metrics table: validation only
    # ============================================================================
    mask = val_df["rul_true"].notna()
    y_true_all = val_df.loc[mask, "rul_true"].to_numpy()

    rows = []
    for method in all_methods:
        rmse, mae, r2 = metrics(y_true_all, val_df.loc[mask, rcol(method)].to_numpy())
        cat = ("Proposed" if method == "Proposed"
               else "Ablation" if method in ["Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model"]
               else "Baseline")
        rows.append({"Method": DISPLAY[method], "Category": cat,
                     "RMSE": rmse, "MAE": mae, "R2": r2})

    metric_df = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("RMSE").reset_index(drop=True)
    metric_df.to_csv(os.path.join(FIG_DIR, "RUL_Metrics_MSSP_RESS.csv"), index=False)

    print("\nRUL validation metrics:")
    print(metric_df.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nLaTeX table rows:")
    for _, r in metric_df.iterrows():
        bold = r["Method"] == "Proposed"
        fmt = (lambda v: f"\\textbf{{{v}}}") if bold else (lambda v: f"{v}")
        print(f"{fmt(r['Method'])} & {fmt(r['Category'])} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['RMSE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['MAE'], '.2f'))} & "
              f"{fmt(format(r['R2'], '.4f'))}" + r" \\")

    print(f"\nAll PNG figures and metrics table saved in:\n{FIG_DIR}")

    # ============================================================================
    # RUL METRICS FROM WEAR TRAJECTORY FILES
    # Works even if rul_trajectories_validation.csv does not exist
    # ============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd

    # ============================================================================
    # PATHS
    # ============================================================================

    RESULTS_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real"
    TRAJ_DIR    = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, "trajectories")
    OUT_DIR     = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, "metrics_tables_RUL_MSSP_RESS")
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    WEAR_VAL_FILE  = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    WEAR_TEST_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_test.csv")

    # ============================================================================
    # SETTINGS
    # ============================================================================

    FAILURE_THRESHOLD = 165.0   # µm, change if your paper uses another threshold

    METHODS = {
        "TCN": "TCN_wear",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM_wear",
        "Transformer": "Transformer_wear",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE_wear",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed_wear",
        "Proposed": "PINN_wear",
    }

    STD_COLS = {
        "TCN": "TCN_wear_std",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM_wear_std",
        "Transformer": "Transformer_wear_std",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE_wear_std",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed_wear_std",
        "Proposed": "PINN_wear_std",
    }

    EPS = 1e-12

    # ============================================================================
    # METRICS
    # ============================================================================

    def rmse(y, p):
        return np.sqrt(np.mean((p - y) ** 2))

    def mae(y, p):
        return np.mean(np.abs(p - y))

    def mape(y, p):
        return np.mean(np.abs((y - p) / (np.abs(y) + EPS))) * 100

    def smape(y, p):
        return np.mean(2 * np.abs(p - y) / (np.abs(y) + np.abs(p) + EPS)) * 100

    def r2(y, p):
        return 1 - np.sum((y - p) ** 2) / (np.sum((y - y.mean()) ** 2) + EPS)

    def evs(y, p):
        return 1 - np.var(y - p) / (np.var(y) + EPS)

    def nasa_score(y, p):
        e = p - y
        score = np.zeros_like(e, dtype=float)
        score[e < 0] = np.exp(-e[e < 0] / 13.0) - 1.0
        score[e >= 0] = np.exp(e[e >= 0] / 10.0) - 1.0
        return np.sum(score)

    def relative_accuracy(y, p):
        return 100 - mape(y, p)

    def picp(y, p, s, z=1.96):
        lo = p - z * s
        hi = p + z * s
        return np.mean((y >= lo) & (y <= hi)) * 100

    def mpiw(s, z=1.96):
        return np.mean(2 * z * s)

    def nll(y, p, s):
        s = np.maximum(s, EPS)
        var = s ** 2
        return np.mean(0.5 * np.log(2 * np.pi * var) + ((y - p) ** 2) / (2 * var))

    # ============================================================================
    # RUL CONVERSION
    # ============================================================================

    def wear_to_rul(cut_numbers, wear_values, threshold=165.0):
        cut_numbers = np.asarray(cut_numbers)
        wear_values = np.asarray(wear_values)

        fail_idx = np.where(wear_values >= threshold)[0]

        if len(fail_idx) > 0:
            fail_cut = cut_numbers[fail_idx[0]]
        else:
            fail_cut = cut_numbers[-1]

        rul = fail_cut - cut_numbers
        rul = np.maximum(rul, 0)

        return rul

    # ============================================================================
    # LOAD DATA
    # ============================================================================

    if not os.path.isfile(WEAR_VAL_FILE):
        raise FileNotFoundError(WEAR_VAL_FILE)

    val_df = pd.read_csv(WEAR_VAL_FILE)

    if os.path.isfile(WEAR_TEST_FILE):
        test_df = pd.read_csv(WEAR_TEST_FILE)
    else:
        test_df = None

    # Only validation has true wear, so true RUL metrics are calculated on validation cutters
    eval_df = val_df[val_df["wear_true"].notna()].copy()

    # ============================================================================
    # BUILD RUL COLUMNS
    # ============================================================================

    all_parts = []

    for cutter, d in eval_df.groupby("cutter"):
        d = d.sort_values("cut_number").copy()
        x = d["cut_number"].to_numpy()

        d["RUL_true"] = wear_to_rul(x, d["wear_true"].to_numpy(), FAILURE_THRESHOLD)

        for method, col in METHODS.items():
            if col in d.columns:
                d[f"{method}_RUL"] = wear_to_rul(x, d[col].to_numpy(), FAILURE_THRESHOLD)

            std_col = STD_COLS.get(method)
            if std_col in d.columns:
                # approximate propagation: wear uncertainty to RUL uncertainty
                d[f"{method}_RUL_std"] = np.maximum(d[std_col].to_numpy(), 1.0)

        all_parts.append(d)

    rul_df = pd.concat(all_parts, ignore_index=True)

    # ============================================================================
    # TABLE 1: DETERMINISTIC RUL PERFORMANCE
    # ============================================================================

    rows = []

    for method in METHODS.keys():
        pred_col = f"{method}_RUL"

        if pred_col not in rul_df.columns:
            continue

        d = rul_df[["RUL_true", pred_col]].dropna()
        y = d["RUL_true"].to_numpy()
        p = d[pred_col].to_numpy()

        rows.append({
            "Method": method,
            "RUL RMSE ↓": rmse(y, p),
            "RUL MAE ↓": mae(y, p),
            "RUL MAPE (%) ↓": mape(y, p),
            "RUL sMAPE (%) ↓": smape(y, p),
            "RUL R² ↑": r2(y, p),
            "RUL EVS ↑": evs(y, p),
            "NASA Score ↓": nasa_score(y, p),
            "Relative Accuracy (%) ↑": relative_accuracy(y, p),
        })

    det_table = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("RUL RMSE ↓").reset_index(drop=True)

    # ============================================================================
    # TABLE 2: RUL UNCERTAINTY
    # ============================================================================

    unc_rows = []

    for method in METHODS.keys():
        pred_col = f"{method}_RUL"
        std_col = f"{method}_RUL_std"

        if pred_col not in rul_df.columns or std_col not in rul_df.columns:
            continue

        d = rul_df[["RUL_true", pred_col, std_col]].dropna()
        y = d["RUL_true"].to_numpy()
        p = d[pred_col].to_numpy()
        s = d[std_col].to_numpy()

        unc_rows.append({
            "Method": method,
            "PICP (%) ↑": picp(y, p, s),
            "MPIW ↓": mpiw(s),
            "NLL ↓": nll(y, p, s),
        })

    unc_table = pd.DataFrame(unc_rows).sort_values("PICP (%) ↑", ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)

    # ============================================================================
    # SAVE
    # ============================================================================

    det_table = det_table.round(4)
    unc_table = unc_table.round(4)

    det_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "Table_RUL_Deterministic_From_Wear.csv")
    unc_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "Table_RUL_Uncertainty_From_Wear.csv")
    xlsx_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "RUL_Metrics_From_Wear.xlsx")

    det_table.to_csv(det_path, index=False)
    unc_table.to_csv(unc_path, index=False)

    with pd.ExcelWriter(xlsx_path, engine="openpyxl") as writer:
        det_table.to_excel(writer, sheet_name="RUL deterministic", index=False)
        unc_table.to_excel(writer, sheet_name="RUL uncertainty", index=False)
        rul_df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name="RUL trajectories", index=False)

    print("\nRUL deterministic metrics:")
    print(det_table.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nRUL uncertainty metrics:")
    print(unc_table.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nSaved:")
    print(det_path)
    print(unc_path)
    print(xlsx_path)


    RUL deterministic metrics:

               Transformer      2.4339  4.2593 756.6599

    Saved:
    E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\metrics_tables_RUL_MSSP_RESS\Table_RUL_Deterministic_From_Wear.csv
    E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\metrics_tables_RUL_MSSP_RESS\Table_RUL_Uncertainty_From_Wear.csv
    E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\metrics_tables_RUL_MSSP_RESS\RUL_Metrics_From_Wear.xlsx

    #additional

    # ============================================================================
    # ADDITIONAL RESULTS SUITE — MSSP / RESS STYLE
    # All figures computed from REAL prediction files. Nothing synthesized.
    #
    # Figures:
    #   A1  Error distribution boxplot (all six methods)
    #   A2  Ground truth vs prediction scatter (Proposed)
    #   A3  Bland–Altman plot (Proposed)
    #   A4  Residual histogram (Proposed)
    #   A5  Calibration / reliability plot (expected vs observed confidence)
    #   A6  Coverage vs mean interval width
    #   A7  Failure-threshold detection error (signed, per cutter + table)
    #   A8  Training convergence (LOADED from history file; skipped if absent)
    #   A9  Attention visualization (LOADED from weights file; skipped if absent)
    #
    # Output: F:\GIST work\Results\additional results  (PNG, 600 DPI)
    # ============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import matplotlib.font_manager as fm
    from math import erf

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- paths
    TRAJ_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\trajectories"

    WEAR_VAL_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    RUL_VAL_FILE  = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "rul_trajectories_validation.csv")

    # Optional inputs — figures A8/A9 are produced ONLY if these exist.
    # Export from your training pipeline:
    #   training_history.csv  -> columns: epoch, train_loss, val_loss
    #   attention_weights.npy -> 2D array [layers_or_heads x positions] or [positions]
    TRAINING_HISTORY_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "training_history.csv")
    ATTENTION_FILE        = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "attention_weights.npy")

    OUT_DIR = r"F:\GIST work\Results\additional results"
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    LOCAL_FALLBACKS = {
        WEAR_VAL_FILE: "wear_trajectories_validation.csv",
        RUL_VAL_FILE: "rul_trajectories_validation.csv",
        TRAINING_HISTORY_FILE: "training_history.csv",
        ATTENTION_FILE: "attention_weights.npy",
    }

    def resolve(path):
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            return path
        alt = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), LOCAL_FALLBACKS.get(path, os.path.basename(path)))
        return alt if os.path.isfile(alt) else path

    WEAR_VAL_FILE = resolve(WEAR_VAL_FILE)
    RUL_VAL_FILE = resolve(RUL_VAL_FILE)
    TRAINING_HISTORY_FILE = resolve(TRAINING_HISTORY_FILE)
    ATTENTION_FILE = resolve(ATTENTION_FILE)

    DPI = 600

    # Wear failure threshold for PHM2010 flank wear, in the SAME UNIT as your
    # wear_true column. Set correctly before running (e.g., 165 if μm, 0.165 if mm).
    WEAR_FAILURE_THRESHOLD = 165.0
    WEAR_UNIT = "μm"   # used in axis labels only — match your data

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- methods
    METHODS = ["TCN", "BiLSTM", "Transformer", "Neural ODE", "Deep State Space Model", "Proposed"]

    WEAR_COL = {
        "TCN": "TCN_wear",
        "BiLSTM": "BiLSTM_wear",
        "Transformer": "Transformer_wear",
        "Neural ODE": "Neural ODE_wear",
        "Deep State Space Model": "Proposed_wear",
        "Proposed": "PINN_wear",
    }
    WEAR_STD_COL = {m: WEAR_COL[m] + "_std" for m in METHODS}

    COLORS = {
        "TCN":                      "#8E7CC3",
        "BiLSTM":                   "#4FA88B",
        "Transformer":              "#4F8FC0",
        "Neural ODE":               "#E2895B",
        "Deep State Space Model":   "#9A9A9A",
        "Proposed":                 "#D7263D",
    }
    TRUTH_COLOR = "#2B2B2B"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- fonts / style
    _avail = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    _family = next((f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in _avail), "serif")
    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "serif",
        "font.serif": [_family],
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 11,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 9,
        "ytick.labelsize": 9,
        "legend.fontsize": 8,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.0,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "pdf.fonttype": 42, "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    def style_axis(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_linewidth(1.0)
        for lbl in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")

    def save_fig(fig, name):
        path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{name}.png")
        fig.savefig(path, dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
        plt.close(fig)
        print(f"Saved: {path}")

    def normal_cdf(x):
        return 0.5 * (1.0 + np.vectorize(erf)(np.asarray(x, dtype=float) / np.sqrt(2)))

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- load data
    print("=" * 80)
    print("ADDITIONAL RESULTS SUITE — computed from real prediction files")
    print("=" * 80)

    wear = pd.read_csv(WEAR_VAL_FILE)
    if "wear_true" not in wear.columns:
        raise ValueError("'wear_true' missing from wear validation file.")

    cutter_col = next((c for c in ["cutter", "Cutter", "cutter_id"] if c in wear.columns), None)
    cut_col = next((c for c in ["cut_number", "cut", "pass"] if c in wear.columns), None)

    available = [m for m in METHODS if WEAR_COL[m] in wear.columns]
    missing = [m for m in METHODS if m not in available]
    if missing:
        print(f"[WARN] Missing wear columns for: {missing} — excluded where needed.")

    def err_of(method):
        d = wear[["wear_true", WEAR_COL[method]]].dropna()
        return (d[WEAR_COL[method]] - d["wear_true"]).to_numpy(dtype=float)

    # ============================================================================
    # A1 — ERROR DISTRIBUTION BOXPLOT (all methods)
    # ============================================================================
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6.2, 3.6))
    data = [err_of(m) for m in available]
    bp = ax.boxplot(data, patch_artist=True, widths=0.55, showfliers=True,
                    flierprops=dict(marker="o", markersize=2.5, alpha=0.45,
                                    markerfacecolor="#777777", markeredgecolor="none"),
                    medianprops=dict(color="black", lw=1.2))
    for patch, m in zip(bp["boxes"], available):
        patch.set_facecolor(COLORS[m])
        patch.set_alpha(0.65)
        patch.set_edgecolor("black")
        patch.set_linewidth(0.8)
    ax.axhline(0, color="black", lw=0.8, alpha=0.6, linestyle="--")
    ax.set_xticklabels([m if m != "Deep State Space Model" else "DSSM" for m in available],
                       rotation=20, ha="right")
    ax.set_ylabel(f"Prediction error ({WEAR_UNIT})")
    style_axis(ax)
    fig.tight_layout()
    save_fig(fig, "A1_Error_Distribution_Boxplot")

    # ============================================================================
    # A2 — GROUND TRUTH vs PREDICTION SCATTER (Proposed)
    # ============================================================================
    if "Proposed" in available:
        d = wear[["wear_true", WEAR_COL["Proposed"]]].dropna()
        y_true = d["wear_true"].to_numpy()
        y_pred = d[WEAR_COL["Proposed"]].to_numpy()
        ss_res = np.sum((y_true - y_pred) ** 2)
        ss_tot = np.sum((y_true - y_true.mean()) ** 2)
        r2 = 1 - ss_res / max(ss_tot, 1e-12)

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(4.0, 4.0))
        lims = [min(y_true.min(), y_pred.min()), max(y_true.max(), y_pred.max())]
        pad = 0.04 * (lims[1] - lims[0])
        lims = [lims[0] - pad, lims[1] + pad]
        ax.plot(lims, lims, "--", color="black", lw=1.0, alpha=0.7, zorder=2,
                label="Ideal (y = x)")
        ax.scatter(y_true, y_pred, s=10, color=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=0.45,
                   edgecolors="none", zorder=3, label="Proposed")
        ax.text(0.05, 0.92, f"$R^2$ = {r2:.4f}", transform=ax.transAxes,
                fontsize=10, fontweight="bold",
                bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.3", facecolor="white",
                          edgecolor="black", lw=0.8))
        ax.set_xlim(lims); ax.set_ylim(lims)
        ax.set_xlabel(f"Ground truth wear ({WEAR_UNIT})")
        ax.set_ylabel(f"Predicted wear ({WEAR_UNIT})")
        ax.set_aspect("equal")
        leg = ax.legend(loc="lower right", frameon=True, edgecolor="black")
        style_axis(ax)
        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, "A2_Scatter_Truth_vs_Prediction_Proposed")

    # ============================================================================
    # A3 — BLAND–ALTMAN PLOT (Proposed)
    # ============================================================================
    if "Proposed" in available:
        d = wear[["wear_true", WEAR_COL["Proposed"]]].dropna()
        y_true = d["wear_true"].to_numpy()
        y_pred = d[WEAR_COL["Proposed"]].to_numpy()
        mean_vals = (y_true + y_pred) / 2.0
        diff_vals = y_pred - y_true
        bias = np.mean(diff_vals)
        sd = np.std(diff_vals, ddof=1)
        loa_hi, loa_lo = bias + 1.96 * sd, bias - 1.96 * sd

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.0, 3.6))
        ax.scatter(mean_vals, diff_vals, s=10, color=COLORS["Proposed"],
                   alpha=0.45, edgecolors="none", zorder=3)
        ax.axhline(bias, color="black", lw=1.2,
                   label=f"Bias = {bias:.3f} {WEAR_UNIT}")
        ax.axhline(loa_hi, color="#555555", lw=1.0, linestyle="--",
                   label=f"±1.96 SD = [{loa_lo:.2f}, {loa_hi:.2f}]")
        ax.axhline(loa_lo, color="#555555", lw=1.0, linestyle="--")
        ax.set_xlabel(f"Mean of truth and prediction ({WEAR_UNIT})")
        ax.set_ylabel(f"Prediction − truth ({WEAR_UNIT})")
        leg = ax.legend(loc="upper right", frameon=True, edgecolor="black")
        style_axis(ax)
        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, "A3_Bland_Altman_Proposed")

    # ============================================================================
    # A4 — RESIDUAL HISTOGRAM (Proposed)
    # ============================================================================
    if "Proposed" in available:
        res = err_of("Proposed")
        mu, sd = np.mean(res), np.std(res, ddof=1)

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(4.6, 3.4))
        ax.hist(res, bins=30, density=True, color=COLORS["Proposed"],
                alpha=0.55, edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.5)
        xs = np.linspace(res.min(), res.max(), 300)
        gauss = np.exp(-0.5 * ((xs - mu) / max(sd, 1e-12)) ** 2) / (max(sd, 1e-12) * np.sqrt(2 * np.pi))
        ax.plot(xs, gauss, color="black", lw=1.3,
                label=f"Normal fit ($\\mu$={mu:.3f}, $\\sigma$={sd:.3f})")
        ax.axvline(0, color="#555555", lw=1.0, linestyle="--")
        ax.set_xlabel(f"Residual ({WEAR_UNIT})")
        ax.set_ylabel("Density")
        leg = ax.legend(loc="upper right", frameon=True, edgecolor="black", fontsize=7.5)
        style_axis(ax)
        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, "A4_Residual_Histogram_Proposed")

    # ============================================================================
    # A5 — CALIBRATION / RELIABILITY PLOT (all methods with std columns)
    # ============================================================================
    conf_levels = np.linspace(0.05, 0.99, 25)
    # two-sided Gaussian z for central interval at each confidence level
    from scipy.stats import norm as _norm  # noqa: E402
    z_levels = _norm.ppf(0.5 + conf_levels / 2.0)

    methods_with_std = [m for m in available if WEAR_STD_COL[m] in wear.columns]

    if methods_with_std:
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(4.2, 4.2))
        ax.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], "--", color="black", lw=1.0, alpha=0.7,
                label="Perfect calibration")
        for m in methods_with_std:
            d = wear[["wear_true", WEAR_COL[m], WEAR_STD_COL[m]]].dropna()
            yt = d["wear_true"].to_numpy()
            yp = d[WEAR_COL[m]].to_numpy()
            ys = np.maximum(d[WEAR_STD_COL[m]].to_numpy(), 1e-12)
            observed = [np.mean(np.abs(yt - yp) <= z * ys) for z in z_levels]
            ax.plot(conf_levels, observed, "-", color=COLORS[m], lw=1.6 if m == "Proposed" else 1.0,
                    alpha=1.0 if m == "Proposed" else 0.85,
                    label=m if m != "Deep State Space Model" else "DSSM")
        ax.set_xlabel("Expected confidence")
        ax.set_ylabel("Observed confidence")
        ax.set_xlim(0, 1); ax.set_ylim(0, 1)
        ax.set_aspect("equal")
        leg = ax.legend(loc="lower right", frameon=True, edgecolor="black", fontsize=7)
        style_axis(ax)
        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, "A5_Calibration_Reliability_Plot")
    else:
        print("[SKIP] A5: no std columns found.")

    # ============================================================================
    # A6 — COVERAGE vs MEAN INTERVAL WIDTH
    # ============================================================================
    if methods_with_std:
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.0, 3.8))
        for m in methods_with_std:
            d = wear[["wear_true", WEAR_COL[m], WEAR_STD_COL[m]]].dropna()
            yt = d["wear_true"].to_numpy()
            yp = d[WEAR_COL[m]].to_numpy()
            ys = np.maximum(d[WEAR_STD_COL[m]].to_numpy(), 1e-12)
            widths, covers = [], []
            for z in z_levels:
                widths.append(np.mean(2 * z * ys))
                covers.append(np.mean(np.abs(yt - yp) <= z * ys) * 100)
            ax.plot(widths, covers, "-", color=COLORS[m],
                    lw=1.6 if m == "Proposed" else 1.0,
                    alpha=1.0 if m == "Proposed" else 0.85,
                    label=m if m != "Deep State Space Model" else "DSSM")
        ax.axhline(95, color="black", lw=0.8, linestyle="--", alpha=0.6)
        ax.text(0.98, 0.955, "95% target", transform=ax.get_yaxis_transform(),
                ha="right", va="bottom", fontsize=7.5, color="#333333")
        ax.set_xlabel(f"Mean interval width ({WEAR_UNIT})")
        ax.set_ylabel("Empirical coverage (%)")
        leg = ax.legend(loc="lower right", frameon=True, edgecolor="black", fontsize=7)
        style_axis(ax)
        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, "A6_Coverage_vs_Interval_Width")

    # ============================================================================
    # A7 — FAILURE-THRESHOLD DETECTION ERROR / TIME-TO-THRESHOLD ERROR
    #      (first crossing of WEAR_FAILURE_THRESHOLD, per cutter)
    # ============================================================================
    def first_crossing(x, y, threshold):
        """First pass index at which y >= threshold, with linear interpolation
        between the bracketing passes. Returns np.nan if never crossed."""
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        idx = np.where(y >= threshold)[0]
        if len(idx) == 0:
            return np.nan
        i = idx[0]
        if i == 0:
            return x[0]
        x0, x1 = x[i - 1], x[i]
        y0, y1 = y[i - 1], y[i]
        if y1 == y0:
            return x1
        return x0 + (threshold - y0) * (x1 - x0) / (y1 - y0)

    tt_rows = []
    if cutter_col is not None and cut_col is not None:
        for cutter, dc in wear.groupby(cutter_col, sort=True):
            dc = dc.sort_values(cut_col)
            x = dc[cut_col].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            t_true = first_crossing(x, dc["wear_true"].to_numpy(), WEAR_FAILURE_THRESHOLD)
            if np.isnan(t_true):
                print(f"[INFO] {cutter}: ground truth never crosses threshold "
                      f"{WEAR_FAILURE_THRESHOLD} {WEAR_UNIT} — check threshold/unit.")
                continue
            row = {"Cutter": str(cutter).upper(), "True crossing (pass)": t_true}
            for m in available:
                t_pred = first_crossing(x, dc[WEAR_COL[m]].to_numpy(), WEAR_FAILURE_THRESHOLD)
                row[m] = t_pred - t_true if not np.isnan(t_pred) else np.nan
            tt_rows.append(row)

    if tt_rows:
        tt_df = pd.DataFrame(tt_rows)
        tt_csv = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "A7_Failure_Threshold_Detection_Error.csv")

        # Summary: mean absolute detection error per method
        summary = {"Method": [], "Mean signed error (passes)": [],
                   "Mean |error| (passes)": []}
        for m in available:
            vals = tt_df[m].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            vals = vals[np.isfinite(vals)]
            summary["Method"].append(m)
            summary["Mean signed error (passes)"].append(np.mean(vals) if len(vals) else np.nan)
            summary["Mean |error| (passes)"].append(np.mean(np.abs(vals)) if len(vals) else np.nan)
        summary_df = pd.DataFrame(summary).sort_values("Mean |error| (passes)")
        tt_df.to_csv(tt_csv, index=False)
        summary_df.to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "A7_Failure_Threshold_Summary.csv"), index=False)

        print("\nFailure-threshold detection error (predicted − true crossing, passes):")
        print(tt_df.to_string(index=False))
        print("\nSummary:")
        print(summary_df.to_string(index=False))

        # Grouped bar figure: signed error per cutter per method
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6.4, 3.6))
        cutters = tt_df["Cutter"].tolist()
        n_m = len(available)
        width = 0.8 / n_m
        xpos = np.arange(len(cutters))
        for j, m in enumerate(available):
            vals = tt_df[m].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            ax.bar(xpos + (j - n_m / 2 + 0.5) * width, vals, width,
                   color=COLORS[m], edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.5,
                   label=m if m != "Deep State Space Model" else "DSSM")
        ax.axhline(0, color="black", lw=0.9)
        ax.set_xticks(xpos)
        ax.set_xticklabels(cutters)
        ax.set_xlabel("Cutter")
        ax.set_ylabel("Threshold detection error (passes)")
        leg = ax.legend(loc="best", frameon=True, edgecolor="black",
                        fontsize=7, ncol=2)
        style_axis(ax)
        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, "A7_Failure_Threshold_Detection_Error")
    else:
        print("[SKIP] A7: needs cutter and cut_number columns, and threshold crossings.")

    # ============================================================================
    # A8 — TRAINING CONVERGENCE (loaded from real history file only)
    # ============================================================================
    if os.path.isfile(TRAINING_HISTORY_FILE):
        hist = pd.read_csv(TRAINING_HISTORY_FILE)
        ep_col = next((c for c in ["epoch", "Epoch"] if c in hist.columns), None)
        tr_col = next((c for c in ["train_loss", "loss", "training_loss"] if c in hist.columns), None)
        va_col = next((c for c in ["val_loss", "validation_loss"] if c in hist.columns), None)
        if ep_col and tr_col:
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(4.8, 3.4))
            ax.plot(hist[ep_col], hist[tr_col], color=COLORS["Proposed"],
                    lw=1.4, label="Training loss")
            if va_col:
                ax.plot(hist[ep_col], hist[va_col], color="#4F8FC0",
                        lw=1.4, linestyle="--", label="Validation loss")
            ax.set_yscale("log")
            ax.set_xlabel("Epoch")
            ax.set_ylabel("Loss (log scale)")
            leg = ax.legend(loc="upper right", frameon=True, edgecolor="black")
            style_axis(ax)
            fig.tight_layout()
            save_fig(fig, "A8_Training_Convergence")
        else:
            print(f"[SKIP] A8: history file found but needs 'epoch' and 'train_loss' columns. "
                  f"Found: {list(hist.columns)}")
    else:
        print("[SKIP] A8: no training_history.csv found. Export it from your training "
              "loop (columns: epoch, train_loss, val_loss). Do NOT hand-draw this curve.")

    # ============================================================================
    # A9 — ATTENTION VISUALIZATION (loaded from real weights file only)
    # ============================================================================
    if os.path.isfile(ATTENTION_FILE):
        attn = np.load(ATTENTION_FILE)
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.4, 3.2))
        if attn.ndim == 1:
            ax.plot(np.arange(len(attn)), attn, color=COLORS["Proposed"], lw=1.4)
            ax.fill_between(np.arange(len(attn)), 0, attn,
                            color=COLORS["Proposed"], alpha=0.25)
            ax.set_xlabel("Input position")
            ax.set_ylabel("Attention weight")
            style_axis(ax)
        else:
            im = ax.imshow(attn, aspect="auto", cmap="viridis", interpolation="nearest")
            cb = fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.03)
            cb.set_label("Attention weight", fontweight="bold")
            ax.set_xlabel("Input position")
            ax.set_ylabel("Head / layer")
            for lbl in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
                lbl.set_fontweight("bold")
        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, "A9_Attention_Visualization")
    else:
        print("[SKIP] A9: no attention_weights.npy found. If your model has attention, "
              "save the weights from a forward pass (np.save) and re-run. If it does "
              "not have attention, omit this figure — do not add a decorative heatmap.")

    print("\nDone. All available figures saved to:")
    print(OUT_DIR)
