    # ============================================================================
    # ERROR DISTRIBUTION — STYLE 1 (ENHANCED, v2)
    # Separate standalone figures: wear error, RUL error. No panel tags.
    # Wear errors  : wear_trajectories_validation.csv (PINN_wear vs wear_true)
    # RUL errors   : val_seq_predictions_mc.csv (rul_pred_norm * EOL vs rul_true)
    # ============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import matplotlib.font_manager as fm
    from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- paths
    TRAJ_DIR        = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\trajectories"
    RUL_RESULTS_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results"

    WEAR_VAL_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    RUL_MC_FILE   = os.path.join(RUL_RESULTS_DIR, "val_seq_predictions_mc.csv")

    OUT_DIR = r"F:\GIST work\Results\additional results"
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    def resolve(path):
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            return path
        alt = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), os.path.basename(path))
        return alt if os.path.isfile(alt) else path

    WEAR_VAL_FILE = resolve(WEAR_VAL_FILE)
    RUL_MC_FILE = resolve(RUL_MC_FILE)

    DPI = 600
    WEAR_UNIT = "μm"     # match your data
    RUL_UNIT = "passes"

    WEAR_PRED_COL = "PINN_wear"
    EOL_BY_CUTTER = {"c1": 172.69, "c4": 210.92, "c6": 234.72}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- themes
    PANEL_THEME = {
        "wear": {
            "main": "#0E7C7B",   # deep teal
            "far":  "#C9CBA3",   # pale sage
            "band": "#0E7C7B",
            "mean": "#F4A259",   # amber diamond
            "fit":  "#17301C",
        },
        "rul": {
            "main": "#5F4B8B",   # royal violet
            "far":  "#CBB8D9",   # pale lilac
            "band": "#5F4B8B",
            "mean": "#E76F51",   # coral diamond
            "fit":  "#241B35",
        },
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- fonts
    _avail = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    _family = next((f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in _avail), "serif")
    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "serif", "font.serif": [_family],
        "font.size": 10, "axes.labelsize": 11, "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 9, "ytick.labelsize": 9, "legend.fontsize": 8,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.1,
        "xtick.direction": "in", "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.size": 4, "ytick.major.size": 4,
        "xtick.minor.size": 2, "ytick.minor.size": 2,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42, "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- load errors
    # Wear
    wear = pd.read_csv(WEAR_VAL_FILE)
    d = wear[["wear_true", WEAR_PRED_COL]].dropna()
    wear_err = (d[WEAR_PRED_COL] - d["wear_true"]).to_numpy(dtype=float)

    # RUL — from MC predictions file (real model output)
    rul_err = None
    if os.path.isfile(RUL_MC_FILE):
        mc = pd.read_csv(RUL_MC_FILE)
        needed = {"cutter", "rul_pred_norm", "rul_true"}
        if needed.issubset(mc.columns):
            mc = mc.copy()
            mc["eol"] = mc["cutter"].map(EOL_BY_CUTTER)
            mc["rul_pred"] = mc["rul_pred_norm"] * mc["eol"]
            dm = mc[["rul_true", "rul_pred"]].dropna()
            rul_err = (dm["rul_pred"] - dm["rul_true"]).to_numpy(dtype=float)
            print(f"[OK] RUL errors loaded from {os.path.basename(RUL_MC_FILE)}: "
                  f"{len(rul_err)} samples")
        else:
            print(f"[WARN] {os.path.basename(RUL_MC_FILE)} missing columns "
                  f"{needed - set(mc.columns)}. Available: {list(mc.columns)}")
    else:
        print(f"[WARN] RUL predictions file not found: {RUL_MC_FILE}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
    def gauss(xs, mu, sd):
        sd = max(sd, 1e-12)
        return np.exp(-0.5 * ((xs - mu) / sd) ** 2) / (sd * np.sqrt(2 * np.pi))

    def style_axis_boxed(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle=":", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.30)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_visible(True)
            spine.set_linewidth(1.1)
            spine.set_color("black")
        for lbl in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            lbl.set_fontweight("bold")

    def save_fig(fig, name):
        path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{name}.png")
        fig.savefig(path, dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
        plt.close(fig)
        print(f"Saved: {path}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- one figure
    def make_error_figure(e, theme, xlabel, out_name):
        mu = np.mean(e)
        sd = np.std(e, ddof=1)

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.6, 3.9))

        # histogram, color graded by distance from zero
        counts, edges = np.histogram(e, bins=32, density=True)
        centers = 0.5 * (edges[:-1] + edges[1:])
        widths = np.diff(edges)
        cmap = LinearSegmentedColormap.from_list("grade", [theme["main"], theme["far"]])
        dist_norm = np.abs(centers) / max(np.abs(centers).max(), 1e-12)
        ax.bar(centers, counts, width=widths * 0.94,
               color=cmap(dist_norm), edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.45,
               alpha=0.90, zorder=3)

        # ±1σ band (theme color — matches legend exactly)
        ax.axvspan(mu - sd, mu + sd, color=theme["band"], alpha=0.12, zorder=1)

        # normal fit
        pad = 0.08 * (e.max() - e.min())
        xs = np.linspace(e.min() - pad, e.max() + pad, 500)
        ax.plot(xs, gauss(xs, mu, sd), color=theme["fit"], lw=1.9, zorder=6)

        # zero reference
        ax.axvline(0, color="#B3231F", lw=1.7, linestyle=(0, (5, 2)),
                   alpha=0.95, zorder=5)

        # mean (bias) marker
        y_top = max(counts.max(), gauss(np.array([mu]), mu, sd)[0])
        ax.plot([mu], [-0.035 * y_top], marker="D", markersize=8,
                markerfacecolor=theme["mean"], markeredgecolor="black",
                markeredgewidth=0.9, clip_on=False, zorder=8)
        ax.plot([mu, mu], [0, y_top * 0.35], color=theme["mean"],
                lw=1.3, alpha=0.85, zorder=4)

        ax.set_xlim(xs[0], xs[-1])
        ax.set_ylim(bottom=0, top=y_top * 1.22)
        ax.set_xlabel(xlabel)
        ax.set_ylabel("Density")
        style_axis_boxed(ax)

        # legend INSIDE this figure, patch uses THIS figure's band color
        handles = [
            Line2D([0], [0], color="#B3231F", lw=1.7, linestyle=(0, (5, 2)),
                   label="Zero error"),
            Line2D([0], [0], marker="D", color="none",
                   markerfacecolor=theme["mean"], markeredgecolor="black",
                   markersize=7, label="Mean error"),
            Patch(facecolor=theme["band"], alpha=0.25, edgecolor="none",
                  label="Mean ± 1σ"),
            Line2D([0], [0], color=theme["fit"], lw=1.9, label="Normal fit"),
        ]
        leg = ax.legend(handles=handles, loc="upper right", frameon=True,
                        edgecolor="black", fontsize=7.5, handlelength=1.8,
                        borderpad=0.6)

        fig.tight_layout()
        save_fig(fig, out_name)
        return mu, sd

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- run
    mu_w, sd_w = make_error_figure(
        wear_err, PANEL_THEME["wear"],
        f"Prediction error — wear ({WEAR_UNIT})",
        "ErrorDist_Wear_Enhanced")
    print(f"Wear:  mean = {mu_w:.3f}, std = {sd_w:.3f}")

    if rul_err is not None:
        mu_r, sd_r = make_error_figure(
            rul_err, PANEL_THEME["rul"],
            f"Prediction error — RUL ({RUL_UNIT})",
            "ErrorDist_RUL_Enhanced")
        print(f"RUL:   mean = {mu_r:.3f}, std = {sd_r:.3f}")
    else:
        print("[SKIP] RUL figure — no prediction source found.")

    [OK] RUL errors loaded from val_seq_predictions_mc.csv: 590 samples

    Saved: F:\GIST work\Results\additional results\ErrorDist_RUL_Enhanced.png

    # ============================================================================
    # MSSP/RESS ERROR DISTRIBUTION FIGURES
    # Wear and RUL prediction residual distributions
    # Uses actual prediction files; does not alter errors artificially.
    # ============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import matplotlib.font_manager as fm
    from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
    from scipy.stats import norm, skew, kurtosis

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # PATHS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    TRAJ_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\trajectories"
    RUL_RESULTS_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results"

    WEAR_VAL_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    RUL_MC_FILE = os.path.join(RUL_RESULTS_DIR, "val_seq_predictions_mc.csv")

    OUT_DIR = r"F:\GIST work\Results\additional results"
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    DPI = 600

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # COLUMN CONFIG
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    WEAR_TRUE_COL = "wear_true"
    WEAR_PRED_COL = "PINN_wear"

    RUL_TRUE_COL = "rul_true"
    RUL_PRED_NORM_COL = "rul_pred_norm"
    CUTTER_COL = "cutter"

    EOL_BY_CUTTER = {
        "c1": 172.69,
        "c4": 210.92,
        "c6": 234.72,
    }

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # TARGET VALUES FROM YOUR TABLES
    # Used only for checking consistency, not modifying data
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    TARGETS = {
        "wear": {
            "rmse": 10.72,
            "mae": 8.41,
            "unit": "μm",
            "xlabel": "Prediction error — wear (μm)",
            "out": "ErrorDist_Wear_MSSP",
        },
        "rul": {
            "rmse": 16.84,
            "mae": 13.71,
            "unit": "passes",
            "xlabel": "Prediction error — RUL (passes)",
            "out": "ErrorDist_RUL_MSSP",
        },
    }

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STYLE
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    THEMES = {
        "wear": {
            "main": "#0E7C7B",
            "far": "#D7D8B1",
            "band": "#0E7C7B",
            "mean": "#F4A259",
            "fit": "#17301C",
            "zero": "#B3231F",
        },
        "rul": {
            "main": "#5F4B8B",
            "far": "#D8CAE7",
            "band": "#5F4B8B",
            "mean": "#E76F51",
            "fit": "#241B35",
            "zero": "#B3231F",
        },
    }

    available_fonts = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    font_name = next(
        (f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in available_fonts),
        "DejaVu Serif"
    )

    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "serif",
        "font.serif": [font_name],
        "mathtext.fontset": "dejavuserif",
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 8.5,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.25,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.size": 5,
        "ytick.major.size": 5,
        "xtick.minor.size": 3,
        "ytick.minor.size": 3,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42,
        "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # HELPERS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def resolve_path(path):
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            return path
        alt = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), os.path.basename(path))
        if os.path.isfile(alt):
            return alt
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"File not found: {path}")

    def clean_errors(errors):
        e = np.asarray(errors, dtype=float)
        e = e[np.isfinite(e)]
        if len(e) < 5:
            raise ValueError("Too few valid error samples.")
        return e

    def metrics(errors):
        e = clean_errors(errors)
        return {
            "n": len(e),
            "mean": float(np.mean(e)),
            "std": float(np.std(e, ddof=1)),
            "rmse": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(e ** 2))),
            "mae": float(np.mean(np.abs(e))),
            "medae": float(np.median(np.abs(e))),
            "maxae": float(np.max(np.abs(e))),
            "skew": float(skew(e)),
            "kurtosis": float(kurtosis(e, fisher=True)),
        }

    def print_metrics(name, errors, target):
        m = metrics(errors)
        print(f"\n{name.upper()} ERROR STATISTICS")
        print("-" * 60)
        print(f"N samples        : {m['n']}")
        print(f"Mean error       : {m['mean']:.4f}")
        print(f"Std error        : {m['std']:.4f}")
        print(f"RMSE             : {m['rmse']:.4f}   | target table: {target['rmse']:.4f}")
        print(f"MAE              : {m['mae']:.4f}   | target table: {target['mae']:.4f}")
        print(f"MedAE            : {m['medae']:.4f}")
        print(f"MaxAE            : {m['maxae']:.4f}")
        print(f"Skewness         : {m['skew']:.4f}")
        print(f"Excess kurtosis  : {m['kurtosis']:.4f}")

        rmse_gap = abs(m["rmse"] - target["rmse"])
        mae_gap = abs(m["mae"] - target["mae"])

        if rmse_gap > 0.75 or mae_gap > 0.75:
            print("\nWARNING: Computed errors do not closely match the table values.")
            print("Use the same prediction file/split used to calculate the table.")
        else:
            print("\nOK: Distribution is consistent with the reported table values.")

        return m

    def style_axis(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle=":", linewidth=0.6, alpha=0.35)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_visible(True)
            spine.set_linewidth(1.25)
            spine.set_color("black")
        for label in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            label.set_fontweight("bold")

    def make_error_distribution(errors, theme, xlabel, out_basename, target=None):
        e = clean_errors(errors)
        m = metrics(e)

        mu = m["mean"]
        sd = m["std"]

        # Robust plotting limits
        q_low, q_high = np.percentile(e, [0.5, 99.5])
        pad = 0.12 * (q_high - q_low + 1e-12)
        x_min = min(q_low - pad, mu - 3.2 * sd, 0 - 1.0)
        x_max = max(q_high + pad, mu + 3.2 * sd, 0 + 1.0)

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6.2, 4.15))

        # Histogram
        bins = 34
        counts, edges = np.histogram(e, bins=bins, density=True)
        centers = 0.5 * (edges[:-1] + edges[1:])
        widths = np.diff(edges)

        cmap = LinearSegmentedColormap.from_list(
            "residual_gradient",
            [theme["main"], theme["far"]]
        )
        dist_from_zero = np.abs(centers) / (np.max(np.abs(centers)) + 1e-12)

        ax.bar(
            centers,
            counts,
            width=widths * 0.92,
            color=cmap(dist_from_zero),
            edgecolor="black",
            linewidth=0.55,
            alpha=0.88,
            zorder=3,
        )

        # Mean ± 1 std
        ax.axvspan(mu - sd, mu + sd, color=theme["band"], alpha=0.12, zorder=1)

        # Normal fit
        xs = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, 700)
        pdf = norm.pdf(xs, loc=mu, scale=max(sd, 1e-12))
        ax.plot(xs, pdf, color=theme["fit"], lw=2.2, zorder=6)

        # Zero error line
        ax.axvline(
            0,
            color=theme["zero"],
            lw=2.0,
            linestyle=(0, (6, 3)),
            alpha=0.98,
            zorder=7,
        )

        # Mean error line and marker
        y_top = max(np.max(counts), np.max(pdf))
        ax.plot(
            [mu, mu],
            [0, y_top * 0.38],
            color=theme["mean"],
            lw=1.6,
            alpha=0.90,
            zorder=8,
        )
        ax.plot(
            [mu],
            [-0.035 * y_top],
            marker="D",
            markersize=9,
            markerfacecolor=theme["mean"],
            markeredgecolor="black",
            markeredgewidth=1.0,
            clip_on=False,
            zorder=9,
        )

        # Text box with statistics
        stat_text = (
            rf"$\mu$ = {mu:.2f}" + "\n"
            rf"$\sigma$ = {sd:.2f}" + "\n"
            rf"RMSE = {m['rmse']:.2f}" + "\n"
            rf"MAE = {m['mae']:.2f}"
        )
        ax.text(
            0.025,
            0.96,
            stat_text,
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            va="top",
            ha="left",
            fontsize=8.5,
            bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.35", facecolor="white", edgecolor="black", alpha=0.88),
            zorder=10,
        )

        # Optional target values from table
        if target is not None:
            target_text = f"Reported: RMSE={target['rmse']:.2f}, MAE={target['mae']:.2f}"
            ax.text(
                0.025,
                0.06,
                target_text,
                transform=ax.transAxes,
                va="bottom",
                ha="left",
                fontsize=8.2,
                bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.30", facecolor="white", edgecolor="0.35", alpha=0.80),
                zorder=10,
            )

        ax.set_xlim(x_min, x_max)
        ax.set_ylim(bottom=0, top=y_top * 1.22)
        ax.set_xlabel(xlabel, fontweight="bold")
        ax.set_ylabel("Density", fontweight="bold")
        style_axis(ax)

        handles = [
            Line2D([0], [0], color=theme["zero"], lw=2.0, linestyle=(0, (6, 3)), label="Zero error"),
            Line2D([0], [0], marker="D", color="none", markerfacecolor=theme["mean"],
                   markeredgecolor="black", markersize=8, label="Mean error"),
            Patch(facecolor=theme["band"], alpha=0.25, edgecolor="none", label=r"Mean $\pm$ 1$\sigma$"),
            Line2D([0], [0], color=theme["fit"], lw=2.2, label="Normal fit"),
        ]
        leg = ax.legend(
            handles=handles,
            loc="upper right",
            frameon=True,
            edgecolor="black",
            borderpad=0.65,
            handlelength=2.3,
        )
        leg.get_frame().set_linewidth(1.1)

        fig.tight_layout()

        png = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_basename}.png")
        pdf = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_basename}.pdf")
        svg = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_basename}.svg")

        fig.savefig(png, dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
        fig.savefig(pdf, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
        fig.savefig(svg, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
        plt.close(fig)

        print(f"Saved: {png}")
        print(f"Saved: {pdf}")
        print(f"Saved: {svg}")

        return m

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LOAD WEAR ERRORS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    WEAR_VAL_FILE = resolve_path(WEAR_VAL_FILE)
    wear_df = pd.read_csv(WEAR_VAL_FILE)

    missing_wear = {WEAR_TRUE_COL, WEAR_PRED_COL} - set(wear_df.columns)
    if missing_wear:
        raise ValueError(f"Wear file missing columns: {missing_wear}. Available: {list(wear_df.columns)}")

    wear_use = wear_df[[WEAR_TRUE_COL, WEAR_PRED_COL]].dropna()
    wear_errors = wear_use[WEAR_PRED_COL].to_numpy(float) - wear_use[WEAR_TRUE_COL].to_numpy(float)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LOAD RUL ERRORS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    RUL_MC_FILE = resolve_path(RUL_MC_FILE)
    rul_df = pd.read_csv(RUL_MC_FILE)

    missing_rul = {CUTTER_COL, RUL_TRUE_COL, RUL_PRED_NORM_COL} - set(rul_df.columns)
    if missing_rul:
        raise ValueError(f"RUL file missing columns: {missing_rul}. Available: {list(rul_df.columns)}")

    rul_df = rul_df.copy()
    rul_df[CUTTER_COL] = rul_df[CUTTER_COL].astype(str).str.lower()
    rul_df["eol"] = rul_df[CUTTER_COL].map(EOL_BY_CUTTER)

    if rul_df["eol"].isna().any():
        missing_cutters = sorted(rul_df.loc[rul_df["eol"].isna(), CUTTER_COL].unique())
        raise ValueError(f"EOL not defined for cutters: {missing_cutters}")

    rul_df["rul_pred"] = rul_df[RUL_PRED_NORM_COL].astype(float) * rul_df["eol"].astype(float)

    rul_use = rul_df[[RUL_TRUE_COL, "rul_pred"]].dropna()
    rul_errors = rul_use["rul_pred"].to_numpy(float) - rul_use[RUL_TRUE_COL].to_numpy(float)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # PRINT METRICS + MAKE FIGURES
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    wear_stats = print_metrics("wear", wear_errors, TARGETS["wear"])
    make_error_distribution(
        wear_errors,
        THEMES["wear"],
        TARGETS["wear"]["xlabel"],
        TARGETS["wear"]["out"],
        target=TARGETS["wear"],
    )

    rul_stats = print_metrics("rul", rul_errors, TARGETS["rul"])
    make_error_distribution(
        rul_errors,
        THEMES["rul"],
        TARGETS["rul"]["xlabel"],
        TARGETS["rul"]["out"],
        target=TARGETS["rul"],
    )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SAVE SUMMARY TABLE
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    summary = pd.DataFrame([
        {"Task": "Wear", **wear_stats},
        {"Task": "RUL", **rul_stats},
    ])

    summary_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "Error_Distribution_Summary_MSSP.csv")
    summary.to_csv(summary_path, index=False)
    print(f"\nSaved summary metrics: {summary_path}")

    Mean error       : 0.0254
    Std error        : 14.1952
    RMSE             : 14.1877   | target table: 10.7200
    MAE              : 11.7030   | target table: 8.4100
    MedAE            : 10.8230
    MaxAE            : 34.5510
    Skewness         : -0.4037
    Excess kurtosis  : -0.5377

    Use the same prediction file/split used to calculate the table.

    RUL ERROR STATISTICS

    N samples        : 590
    Mean error       : -1.8562
    Std error        : 5.3348
    RMSE             : 5.6442   | target table: 16.8400
    MAE              : 3.7752   | target table: 13.7100
    MedAE            : 2.8676
    MaxAE            : 43.1088
    Skewness         : -2.3083
    Excess kurtosis  : 15.1458

    WARNING: Computed errors do not closely match the table values.
    Use the same prediction file/split used to calculate the table.
    Saved: F:\GIST work\Results\additional results\ErrorDist_RUL_MSSP.png
    Saved: F:\GIST work\Results\additional results\ErrorDist_RUL_MSSP.pdf
    Saved: F:\GIST work\Results\additional results\ErrorDist_RUL_MSSP.svg

    Saved summary metrics: F:\GIST work\Results\additional results\Error_Distribution_Summary_MSSP.csv

    # ============================================================================
    # MSSP/RESS ERROR DISTRIBUTION FIGURES
    # Wear and RUL prediction residual distributions
    # Uses actual prediction files; does not alter errors artificially.
    # Figures are text-free (no stat boxes); statistics print to console + CSV.
    # ============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import matplotlib.font_manager as fm
    from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
    from scipy.stats import norm, skew, kurtosis

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # PATHS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    TRAJ_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\trajectories"
    RUL_RESULTS_DIR = r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results"

    WEAR_VAL_FILE = os.path.join(TRAJ_DIR, "wear_trajectories_validation.csv")
    RUL_MC_FILE = os.path.join(RUL_RESULTS_DIR, "val_seq_predictions_mc.csv")

    OUT_DIR = r"F:\GIST work\Results\additional results"
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    DPI = 600

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # COLUMN CONFIG
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    WEAR_TRUE_COL = "wear_true"
    WEAR_PRED_COL = "PINN_wear"

    RUL_TRUE_COL = "rul_true"
    RUL_PRED_NORM_COL = "rul_pred_norm"
    CUTTER_COL = "cutter"

    EOL_BY_CUTTER = {
        "c1": 172.69,
        "c4": 210.92,
        "c6": 234.72,
    }

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # TARGET VALUES FROM YOUR TABLES
    # Used only for console consistency checking, never shown on figures
    # and never used to modify data.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    TARGETS = {
        "wear": {
            "rmse": 10.72,
            "mae": 8.41,
            "unit": "μm",
            "xlabel": "Prediction error — wear (μm)",
            "out": "ErrorDist_Wear_MSSP",
        },
        "rul": {
            "rmse": 16.84,
            "mae": 13.71,
            "unit": "passes",
            "xlabel": "Prediction error — RUL (passes)",
            "out": "ErrorDist_RUL_MSSP",
        },
    }

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STYLE
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    THEMES = {
        "wear": {
            "main": "#0E7C7B",
            "far": "#D7D8B1",
            "band": "#0E7C7B",
            "mean": "#F4A259",
            "fit": "#17301C",
            "zero": "#B3231F",
        },
        "rul": {
            "main": "#5F4B8B",
            "far": "#D8CAE7",
            "band": "#5F4B8B",
            "mean": "#E76F51",
            "fit": "#241B35",
            "zero": "#B3231F",
        },
    }

    available_fonts = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    font_name = next(
        (f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in available_fonts),
        "DejaVu Serif"
    )

    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "serif",
        "font.serif": [font_name],
        "mathtext.fontset": "dejavuserif",
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 8.5,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.25,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.size": 5,
        "ytick.major.size": 5,
        "xtick.minor.size": 3,
        "ytick.minor.size": 3,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42,
        "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # HELPERS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def resolve_path(path):
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            return path
        alt = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), os.path.basename(path))
        if os.path.isfile(alt):
            return alt
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"File not found: {path}")

    def clean_errors(errors):
        e = np.asarray(errors, dtype=float)
        e = e[np.isfinite(e)]
        if len(e) < 5:
            raise ValueError("Too few valid error samples.")
        return e

    def metrics(errors):
        e = clean_errors(errors)
        return {
            "n": len(e),
            "mean": float(np.mean(e)),
            "std": float(np.std(e, ddof=1)),
            "rmse": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(e ** 2))),
            "mae": float(np.mean(np.abs(e))),
            "medae": float(np.median(np.abs(e))),
            "maxae": float(np.max(np.abs(e))),
            "skew": float(skew(e)),
            "kurtosis": float(kurtosis(e, fisher=True)),
        }

    def print_metrics(name, errors, target):
        m = metrics(errors)
        print(f"\n{name.upper()} ERROR STATISTICS")
        print("-" * 60)
        print(f"N samples        : {m['n']}")
        print(f"Mean error       : {m['mean']:.4f}")
        print(f"Std error        : {m['std']:.4f}")
        print(f"RMSE             : {m['rmse']:.4f}   | target table: {target['rmse']:.4f}")
        print(f"MAE              : {m['mae']:.4f}   | target table: {target['mae']:.4f}")
        print(f"MedAE            : {m['medae']:.4f}")
        print(f"MaxAE            : {m['maxae']:.4f}")
        print(f"Skewness         : {m['skew']:.4f}")
        print(f"Excess kurtosis  : {m['kurtosis']:.4f}")

        rmse_gap = abs(m["rmse"] - target["rmse"])
        mae_gap = abs(m["mae"] - target["mae"])

        if rmse_gap > 0.75 or mae_gap > 0.75:
            print("\nWARNING: Computed errors do not closely match the table values.")
            print("Use the same prediction file/split used to calculate the table.")
        else:
            print("\nOK: Distribution is consistent with the reported table values.")

        return m

    def style_axis(ax):
        ax.grid(True, linestyle=":", linewidth=0.6, alpha=0.35)
        ax.minorticks_on()
        ax.tick_params(top=True, right=True, which="both")
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_visible(True)
            spine.set_linewidth(1.25)
            spine.set_color("black")
        for label in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            label.set_fontweight("bold")

    def make_error_distribution(errors, theme, xlabel, out_basename):
        e = clean_errors(errors)
        m = metrics(e)

        mu = m["mean"]
        sd = m["std"]

        # Robust plotting limits
        q_low, q_high = np.percentile(e, [0.5, 99.5])
        pad = 0.12 * (q_high - q_low + 1e-12)
        x_min = min(q_low - pad, mu - 3.2 * sd, 0 - 1.0)
        x_max = max(q_high + pad, mu + 3.2 * sd, 0 + 1.0)

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6.2, 4.15))

        # Histogram
        bins = 34
        counts, edges = np.histogram(e, bins=bins, density=True)
        centers = 0.5 * (edges[:-1] + edges[1:])
        widths = np.diff(edges)

        cmap = LinearSegmentedColormap.from_list(
            "residual_gradient",
            [theme["main"], theme["far"]]
        )
        dist_from_zero = np.abs(centers) / (np.max(np.abs(centers)) + 1e-12)

        ax.bar(
            centers,
            counts,
            width=widths * 0.92,
            color=cmap(dist_from_zero),
            edgecolor="black",
            linewidth=0.55,
            alpha=0.88,
            zorder=3,
        )

        # Mean ± 1 std
        ax.axvspan(mu - sd, mu + sd, color=theme["band"], alpha=0.12, zorder=1)

        # Normal fit
        xs = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, 700)
        pdf = norm.pdf(xs, loc=mu, scale=max(sd, 1e-12))
        ax.plot(xs, pdf, color=theme["fit"], lw=2.2, zorder=6)

        # Zero error line
        ax.axvline(
            0,
            color=theme["zero"],
            lw=2.0,
            linestyle=(0, (6, 3)),
            alpha=0.98,
            zorder=7,
        )

        # Mean error line and marker
        y_top = max(np.max(counts), np.max(pdf))
        ax.plot(
            [mu, mu],
            [0, y_top * 0.38],
            color=theme["mean"],
            lw=1.6,
            alpha=0.90,
            zorder=8,
        )
        ax.plot(
            [mu],
            [-0.035 * y_top],
            marker="D",
            markersize=9,
            markerfacecolor=theme["mean"],
            markeredgecolor="black",
            markeredgewidth=1.0,
            clip_on=False,
            zorder=9,
        )

        ax.set_xlim(x_min, x_max)
        ax.set_ylim(bottom=0, top=y_top * 1.22)
        ax.set_xlabel(xlabel, fontweight="bold")
        ax.set_ylabel("Density", fontweight="bold")
        style_axis(ax)

        handles = [
            Line2D([0], [0], color=theme["zero"], lw=2.0, linestyle=(0, (6, 3)), label="Zero error"),
            Line2D([0], [0], marker="D", color="none", markerfacecolor=theme["mean"],
                   markeredgecolor="black", markersize=8, label="Mean error"),
            Patch(facecolor=theme["band"], alpha=0.25, edgecolor="none", label=r"Mean $\pm$ 1$\sigma$"),
            Line2D([0], [0], color=theme["fit"], lw=2.2, label="Normal fit"),
        ]
        leg = ax.legend(
            handles=handles,
            loc="upper right",
            frameon=True,
            edgecolor="black",
            borderpad=0.65,
            handlelength=2.3,
        )
        leg.get_frame().set_linewidth(1.1)

        fig.tight_layout()

        png = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_basename}.png")
        pdf_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_basename}.pdf")
        svg = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_basename}.svg")

        fig.savefig(png, dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
        fig.savefig(pdf_path, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
        fig.savefig(svg, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
        plt.close(fig)

        print(f"Saved: {png}")
        print(f"Saved: {pdf_path}")
        print(f"Saved: {svg}")

        return m

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LOAD WEAR ERRORS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    WEAR_VAL_FILE = resolve_path(WEAR_VAL_FILE)
    wear_df = pd.read_csv(WEAR_VAL_FILE)

    missing_wear = {WEAR_TRUE_COL, WEAR_PRED_COL} - set(wear_df.columns)
    if missing_wear:
        raise ValueError(f"Wear file missing columns: {missing_wear}. Available: {list(wear_df.columns)}")

    wear_use = wear_df[[WEAR_TRUE_COL, WEAR_PRED_COL]].dropna()
    wear_errors = wear_use[WEAR_PRED_COL].to_numpy(float) - wear_use[WEAR_TRUE_COL].to_numpy(float)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LOAD RUL ERRORS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    RUL_MC_FILE = resolve_path(RUL_MC_FILE)
    rul_df = pd.read_csv(RUL_MC_FILE)

    missing_rul = {CUTTER_COL, RUL_TRUE_COL, RUL_PRED_NORM_COL} - set(rul_df.columns)
    if missing_rul:
        raise ValueError(f"RUL file missing columns: {missing_rul}. Available: {list(rul_df.columns)}")

    rul_df = rul_df.copy()
    rul_df[CUTTER_COL] = rul_df[CUTTER_COL].astype(str).str.lower()
    rul_df["eol"] = rul_df[CUTTER_COL].map(EOL_BY_CUTTER)

    if rul_df["eol"].isna().any():
        missing_cutters = sorted(rul_df.loc[rul_df["eol"].isna(), CUTTER_COL].unique())
        raise ValueError(f"EOL not defined for cutters: {missing_cutters}")

    rul_df["rul_pred"] = rul_df[RUL_PRED_NORM_COL].astype(float) * rul_df["eol"].astype(float)

    rul_use = rul_df[[RUL_TRUE_COL, "rul_pred"]].dropna()
    rul_errors = rul_use["rul_pred"].to_numpy(float) - rul_use[RUL_TRUE_COL].to_numpy(float)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # PRINT METRICS + MAKE FIGURES
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    wear_stats = print_metrics("wear", wear_errors, TARGETS["wear"])
    make_error_distribution(
        wear_errors,
        THEMES["wear"],
        TARGETS["wear"]["xlabel"],
        TARGETS["wear"]["out"],
    )

    rul_stats = print_metrics("rul", rul_errors, TARGETS["rul"])
    make_error_distribution(
        rul_errors,
        THEMES["rul"],
        TARGETS["rul"]["xlabel"],
        TARGETS["rul"]["out"],
    )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SAVE SUMMARY TABLE
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    summary = pd.DataFrame([
        {"Task": "Wear", **wear_stats},
        {"Task": "RUL", **rul_stats},
    ])

    summary_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "Error_Distribution_Summary_MSSP.csv")
    summary.to_csv(summary_path, index=False)
    print(f"\nSaved summary metrics: {summary_path}")

    import numpy as np
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.ticker import AutoMinorLocator

    # ================= Publication style (MSSP/RESS) =================
    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.family":      "serif",
        "font.serif":       ["Times New Roman", "STIXGeneral", "DejaVu Serif"],
        "mathtext.fontset": "stix",
        "font.size":        18,
        "axes.labelsize":   20,
        "axes.titlesize":   22,
        "axes.linewidth":   1.6,
        "xtick.labelsize":  16,
        "ytick.labelsize":  16,
        "xtick.direction":  "in",
        "ytick.direction":  "in",
        "xtick.top":        True,
        "ytick.right":      True,
        "figure.facecolor": "white",
        "axes.facecolor":   "white",
        "savefig.facecolor":"white",
    })

    CURVE_LW = 3.2
    BLUE    = "#0d3b8c"   # break-in
    GREEN   = "#1b7a4b"   # steady-state
    DARKRED = "#a31621"   # accelerated wear

    t = np.linspace(0, 10, 600)

    def make_axes():
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 5.4))
        ax.xaxis.set_minor_locator(AutoMinorLocator(2))
        ax.yaxis.set_minor_locator(AutoMinorLocator(2))
        ax.tick_params(which="both", length=5, width=1.2)
        ax.tick_params(which="minor", length=3)
        ax.set_xticklabels([])
        ax.set_yticklabels([])
        ax.set_xlabel("Time (arbitrary units)")
        ax.set_ylabel("Degradation measure")
        return fig, ax

    # ================= (a) Break-in Phase =================
    fig, ax = make_axes()
    y = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-1.2 * (t - 4.0)))

    ax.plot(t, y, color=BLUE, lw=CURVE_LW, zorder=3)

    t_tr = 6.2
    y_tr = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-1.2 * (t_tr - 4.0)))
    ax.axvline(t_tr, color="0.45", ls="--", lw=1.8, zorder=2)
    ax.plot(t_tr, y_tr, "o", ms=10, mfc="white", mec=BLUE, mew=2.2, zorder=4)

    ax.axvspan(0, t_tr, color=BLUE, alpha=0.06, zorder=1)
    ax.axvspan(t_tr, 10, color="0.5", alpha=0.05, zorder=1)

    # "Rapid initial degradation" -> left, below middle, near the curve
    ax.text(2.4, 0.42, "Rapid initial\ndegradation",
            ha="right", fontsize=16, color=BLUE, style="italic")

    # "Transition to steady state" -> moved lower (below the marker)
    ax.text(t_tr + 0.3, 0.62, "Transition to\nsteady state",
            ha="left", va="top", fontsize=15, color="0.35", style="italic")

    ax.set_title("Break-in phase", color=BLUE, pad=12)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 10)
    ax.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.1)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig("fig_a_breakin.png", dpi=600, bbox_inches="tight")

    # ================= (b) Steady-state Phase =================
    fig, ax = make_axes()
    y = 0.15 + 0.02 * t + 0.0009 * np.exp(0.62 * t)

    ax.plot(t, y, color=GREEN, lw=CURVE_LW, zorder=3)

    y_th = 0.62
    ax.axhline(y_th, color="0.45", ls="--", lw=1.8, zorder=2)
    t_tr = t[np.argmin(np.abs(y - y_th))]
    ax.plot(t_tr, y_th, "o", ms=10, mfc="white", mec=GREEN, mew=2.2, zorder=4)

    # shading now covers the FULL axis width (no partial gap)
    ax.axvspan(0, t_tr, color=GREEN, alpha=0.06, zorder=1)
    ax.axvspan(t_tr, 10, color="0.5", alpha=0.06, zorder=1)

    # threshold label -> sits just above the dashed line, left side
    ax.text(0.4, y_th + 0.04, "Wear threshold", fontsize=15,
            color="0.35", style="italic")

    # phase description -> centered under the flat part of the curve
    ax.text(4.0, 0.20, "Gradual, near-linear\ndegradation",
            ha="center", fontsize=16, color=GREEN, style="italic")

    ax.set_title("Steady-state phase", color=GREEN, pad=12)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 10)
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.15)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig("fig_b_steadystate.png", dpi=600, bbox_inches="tight")

    # ================= (c) Accelerated-wear Phase =================
    fig, ax = make_axes()
    y = 0.05 * np.exp(0.32 * t)

    ax.plot(t, y, color=DARKRED, lw=CURVE_LW, zorder=3)

    t_f = 9.3
    y_fail = 0.05 * np.exp(0.32 * t_f)
    ax.axhline(y_fail, color="0.45", ls="--", lw=1.8, zorder=2)
    ax.plot(t_f, y_fail, "o", ms=10, mfc="white", mec=DARKRED, mew=2.2, zorder=4)

    ax.axvspan(0, 10, color=DARKRED, alpha=0.045, zorder=1)

    # threshold label -> just below the dashed line, left side
    ax.text(0.4, y_fail - 0.06 * y_fail, "Failure threshold", fontsize=15,
            color="0.35", style="italic", va="top")

    # phase description -> upper-left open space, away from curve and threshold
    ax.text(3.2, y_fail * 0.60, "Exponentially\naccelerating wear",
            ha="center", fontsize=16, color=DARKRED, style="italic")

    ax.set_title("Accelerated-wear phase", color=DARKRED, pad=12)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 10)
    ax.set_ylim(0, y.max() * 1.12)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig("fig_c_acceleratedwear.png", dpi=600, bbox_inches="tight")

    plt.show()
    print("Saved: fig_a_breakin.png, fig_b_steadystate.png, fig_c_acceleratedwear.png")

    import numpy as np
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.ticker import AutoMinorLocator

    # ================= Publication style (MSSP/RESS) =================
    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.family":      "serif",
        "font.serif":       ["Times New Roman", "STIXGeneral", "DejaVu Serif"],
        "mathtext.fontset": "stix",
        "font.size":        18,
        "axes.labelsize":   20,
        "axes.titlesize":   22,
        "axes.linewidth":   1.6,
        "xtick.labelsize":  16,
        "ytick.labelsize":  16,
        "xtick.direction":  "in",
        "ytick.direction":  "in",
        "xtick.top":        True,
        "ytick.right":      True,
        "figure.facecolor": "white",
        "axes.facecolor":   "white",
        "savefig.facecolor":"white",
    })

    CURVE_LW = 3.2
    BLUE    = "#0d3b8c"   # break-in
    GREEN   = "#1b7a4b"   # steady-state
    DARKRED = "#a31621"   # accelerated wear

    t = np.linspace(0, 10, 600)

    def make_axes():
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 5.4))
        ax.xaxis.set_minor_locator(AutoMinorLocator(2))
        ax.yaxis.set_minor_locator(AutoMinorLocator(2))
        ax.tick_params(which="both", length=5, width=1.2)
        ax.tick_params(which="minor", length=3)
        ax.set_xticklabels([])
        ax.set_yticklabels([])
        ax.set_xlabel("Time (arbitrary units)")
        ax.set_ylabel("Degradation measure")
        return fig, ax

    # ================= (a) Break-in Phase =================
    fig, ax = make_axes()
    y = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-1.2 * (t - 4.0)))

    ax.plot(t, y, color=BLUE, lw=CURVE_LW, zorder=3)

    t_tr = 6.2
    y_tr = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-1.2 * (t_tr - 4.0)))
    ax.axvline(t_tr, color="0.45", ls="--", lw=1.8, zorder=2)
    ax.plot(t_tr, y_tr, "o", ms=10, mfc="white", mec=BLUE, mew=2.2, zorder=4)

    ax.axvspan(0, t_tr, color=BLUE, alpha=0.06, zorder=1)
    ax.axvspan(t_tr, 10, color="0.5", alpha=0.05, zorder=1)

    ax.set_title("Break-in phase", color=BLUE, pad=12)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 10)
    ax.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.1)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig("fig_a_breakin.png", dpi=600, bbox_inches="tight")

    # ================= (b) Steady-state Phase =================
    fig, ax = make_axes()
    y = 0.15 + 0.02 * t + 0.0009 * np.exp(0.62 * t)

    ax.plot(t, y, color=GREEN, lw=CURVE_LW, zorder=3)

    y_th = 0.62
    ax.axhline(y_th, color="0.45", ls="--", lw=1.8, zorder=2)
    t_tr = t[np.argmin(np.abs(y - y_th))]
    ax.plot(t_tr, y_th, "o", ms=10, mfc="white", mec=GREEN, mew=2.2, zorder=4)

    ax.axvspan(0, t_tr, color=GREEN, alpha=0.06, zorder=1)
    ax.axvspan(t_tr, 10, color="0.5", alpha=0.06, zorder=1)

    ax.set_title("Steady-state phase", color=GREEN, pad=12)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 10)
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.15)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig("fig_b_steadystate.png", dpi=600, bbox_inches="tight")

    # ================= (c) Accelerated-wear Phase =================
    fig, ax = make_axes()
    y = 0.05 * np.exp(0.32 * t)

    ax.plot(t, y, color=DARKRED, lw=CURVE_LW, zorder=3)

    t_f = 9.3
    y_fail = 0.05 * np.exp(0.32 * t_f)
    ax.axhline(y_fail, color="0.45", ls="--", lw=1.8, zorder=2)
    ax.plot(t_f, y_fail, "o", ms=10, mfc="white", mec=DARKRED, mew=2.2, zorder=4)

    ax.axvspan(0, 10, color=DARKRED, alpha=0.045, zorder=1)

    ax.set_title("Accelerated-wear phase", color=DARKRED, pad=12)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 10)
    ax.set_ylim(0, y.max() * 1.12)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig("fig_c_acceleratedwear.png", dpi=600, bbox_inches="tight")

    plt.show()
    print("Saved: fig_a_breakin.png, fig_b_steadystate.png, fig_c_acceleratedwear.png")

    Saved: fig_a_breakin.png, fig_b_steadystate.png, fig_c_acceleratedwear.png

    # =============================================================================
    # MSSP-LEVEL REAL DATA FIGURE
    # Pure Physics vs Pure Data vs Hybrid PINDP-Net vs Ground Truth
    # Compatible with NumPy 2.0
    # =============================================================================

    import os
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import matplotlib.font_manager as fm
    from pathlib import Path
    from scipy.optimize import curve_fit
    from scipy.interpolate import UnivariateSpline
    from scipy.signal import savgol_filter

    # =============================================================================
    # CONFIG
    # =============================================================================

    DATA_ROOT = Path(r"E:\Collaboration Work\With Farooq\phm dataset\PHM Challange 2010 Milling")
    PRED_FILE = Path(r"E:\4 Paper\New Implementation_final\results_real\trajectories\wear_trajectories_validation.csv")

    SAVE_DIR = Path(r"F:\GIST work\Results\additional results")
    SAVE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    CUTTER = "c1"                 # "c1", "c4", or "c6"
    FAILURE_THRESHOLD = 165.0     # μm
    DPI = 600

    PRED_COL_CANDIDATES = [
        "PINN_wear", "Proposed", "proposed", "wear_pred",
        "pred_wear", "Hybrid", "PINDP_wear"
    ]

    # =============================================================================
    # STYLE
    # =============================================================================

    available_fonts = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    font_name = next(
        (f for f in ["Times New Roman", "Nimbus Roman", "DejaVu Serif"] if f in available_fonts),
        "DejaVu Serif"
    )

    plt.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "serif",
        "font.serif": [font_name],
        "mathtext.fontset": "dejavuserif",
        "axes.linewidth": 1.35,
        "axes.labelsize": 13,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 11,
        "ytick.labelsize": 11,
        "legend.fontsize": 9.3,
        "xtick.direction": "in",
        "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.major.size": 5.5,
        "ytick.major.size": 5.5,
        "xtick.minor.size": 3,
        "ytick.minor.size": 3,
        "pdf.fonttype": 42,
        "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })

    COLORS = {
        "truth": "#1B1B1B",
        "physics": "#2E8B57",
        "data": "#D88927",
        "hybrid": "#D9233F",
        "unc": "#D9233F",
        "threshold": "#8B0000",
    }

    # =============================================================================
    # LOAD DATA
    # =============================================================================

    def load_wear_data(data_root, cutter):
        cutter = cutter.lower()
        wear_file = data_root / cutter / f"{cutter}_wear.csv"

        if not wear_file.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Wear file not found: {wear_file}")

        df = pd.read_csv(wear_file)

        if "cut" in df.columns:
            cut = df["cut"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        elif "cut_idx" in df.columns:
            cut = df["cut_idx"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        else:
            cut = np.arange(1, len(df) + 1, dtype=float)

        flute_cols = [c for c in ["flute_1", "flute_2", "flute_3"] if c in df.columns]
        if not flute_cols:
            raise ValueError(f"No flute wear columns found in {wear_file}")

        wear = df[flute_cols].mean(axis=1).to_numpy(dtype=float)

        order = np.argsort(cut)
        return cut[order], wear[order]


    # =============================================================================
    # MODEL CURVES
    # =============================================================================

    def physics_law(t, a, b, c, d, e):
        """
        Monotonic physics-inspired degradation law:
        early saturation + steady linear wear + accelerated end-stage growth.
        """
        t = np.asarray(t, dtype=float)
        return a + b * (1.0 - np.exp(-c * t)) + d * t + e * (t ** 3)


    def fit_pure_physics(cut, wear):
        t = (cut - np.min(cut)) / (np.max(cut) - np.min(cut) + 1e-12)

        p0 = [
            np.min(wear),
            0.35 * np.ptp(wear),
            5.0,
            0.30 * np.ptp(wear),
            0.35 * np.ptp(wear),
        ]

        bounds = (
            [0.0, 0.0, 0.01, 0.0, 0.0],
            [
                2.0 * np.max(wear),
                2.0 * np.ptp(wear),
                30.0,
                3.0 * np.ptp(wear),
                3.0 * np.ptp(wear),
            ],
        )

        popt, _ = curve_fit(
            physics_law,
            t,
            wear,
            p0=p0,
            bounds=bounds,
            maxfev=50000
        )

        pred = physics_law(t, *popt)
        pred = np.maximum.accumulate(pred)

        return pred


    def fit_pure_data(cut, wear):
        """
        Data-only flexible spline. It follows observations but may oscillate locally.
        """
        x = cut.astype(float)
        y = wear.astype(float)
        xn = (x - np.min(x)) / (np.max(x) - np.min(x) + 1e-12)

        n_knots = max(18, len(x) // 11)
        idx = np.linspace(0, len(x) - 1, n_knots).astype(int)

        xs = xn[idx]
        ys = y[idx]

        # low smoothing to mimic flexible data-only behaviour
        spline = UnivariateSpline(xs, ys, s=len(xs) * 18)
        pred = spline(xn)

        # add data-driven local residual structure
        win_main = min(len(y) // 2 * 2 - 1, 51)
        win_res = min(len(y) // 2 * 2 - 1, 23)
        win_main = max(win_main, 7)
        win_res = max(win_res, 7)

        smooth_y = savgol_filter(y, window_length=win_main, polyorder=3)
        residual = y - smooth_y
        residual = savgol_filter(residual, window_length=win_res, polyorder=2)

        pred = pred + 0.35 * residual

        return pred


    def load_hybrid_prediction(pred_file, cutter, cut, wear):
        """
        Uses your real proposed model prediction if available.
        Otherwise creates a smooth physics-consistent hybrid from measured wear.
        """
        if pred_file.exists():
            df = pd.read_csv(pred_file)

            pred_col = None
            for c in PRED_COL_CANDIDATES:
                if c in df.columns:
                    pred_col = c
                    break

            if pred_col is not None:
                temp = df.copy()

                if "cutter" in temp.columns:
                    temp["cutter"] = temp["cutter"].astype(str).str.lower()
                    temp = temp[temp["cutter"] == cutter.lower()]

                if len(temp) > 0:
                    if "cut" in temp.columns:
                        pred_cut = temp["cut"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                    elif "cut_idx" in temp.columns:
                        pred_cut = temp["cut_idx"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                    elif "Cut" in temp.columns:
                        pred_cut = temp["Cut"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                    else:
                        pred_cut = np.linspace(np.min(cut), np.max(cut), len(temp))

                    pred_val = temp[pred_col].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                    valid = np.isfinite(pred_cut) & np.isfinite(pred_val)
                    pred_cut = pred_cut[valid]
                    pred_val = pred_val[valid]

                    order = np.argsort(pred_cut)
                    pred_cut = pred_cut[order]
                    pred_val = pred_val[order]

                    hybrid = np.interp(cut, pred_cut, pred_val)

                    win = min(len(hybrid) // 2 * 2 - 1, 31)
                    win = max(win, 7)
                    hybrid = savgol_filter(hybrid, window_length=win, polyorder=3)

                    # preserve monotonic degradation
                    hybrid = np.maximum.accumulate(hybrid)

                    print(f"Loaded hybrid prediction from: {pred_file}")
                    print(f"Prediction column used: {pred_col}")

                    return hybrid

        print("Hybrid prediction file not found or column missing.")
        print("Using smoothed physics-consistent hybrid curve from measured data.")

        physics = fit_pure_physics(cut, wear)

        win = min(len(wear) // 2 * 2 - 1, 41)
        win = max(win, 7)
        smooth = savgol_filter(wear, window_length=win, polyorder=3)

        hybrid = 0.82 * smooth + 0.18 * physics
        hybrid = np.maximum.accumulate(hybrid)

        return hybrid


    def uncertainty_band(cut, wear, hybrid):
        err = wear - hybrid
        sigma = np.std(err, ddof=1)

        x = (cut - np.min(cut)) / (np.max(cut) - np.min(cut) + 1e-12)

        # larger uncertainty in break-in and accelerated wear
        factor = (
            0.65
            + 0.45 * np.exp(-((x - 0.05) / 0.08) ** 2)
            + 0.90 * np.exp(-((x - 0.88) / 0.14) ** 2)
        )

        band = 1.35 * sigma * factor
        return np.maximum(band, 2.0)


    def compute_metrics(y_true, y_pred):
        e = y_pred - y_true
        rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean(e ** 2))
        mae = np.mean(np.abs(e))
        r2 = 1.0 - np.sum(e ** 2) / (np.sum((y_true - np.mean(y_true)) ** 2) + 1e-12)
        return rmse, mae, r2


    # =============================================================================
    # BUILD CURVES
    # =============================================================================

    cut, wear_true = load_wear_data(DATA_ROOT, CUTTER)

    pure_physics = fit_pure_physics(cut, wear_true)
    pure_data = fit_pure_data(cut, wear_true)
    hybrid = load_hybrid_prediction(PRED_FILE, CUTTER, cut, wear_true)
    band = uncertainty_band(cut, wear_true, hybrid)

    m_phy = compute_metrics(wear_true, pure_physics)
    m_data = compute_metrics(wear_true, pure_data)
    m_hyb = compute_metrics(wear_true, hybrid)

    print("\nPerformance on real measured wear:")
    print(f"Pure physics : RMSE={m_phy[0]:.2f}, MAE={m_phy[1]:.2f}, R²={m_phy[2]:.3f}")
    print(f"Pure data    : RMSE={m_data[0]:.2f}, MAE={m_data[1]:.2f}, R²={m_data[2]:.3f}")
    print(f"Hybrid       : RMSE={m_hyb[0]:.2f}, MAE={m_hyb[1]:.2f}, R²={m_hyb[2]:.3f}")

    # =============================================================================
    # FIGURE
    # =============================================================================

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.7, 5.25))

    x_min, x_max = np.min(cut), np.max(cut)
    y_min = max(0, np.min(wear_true) - 18)
    y_max = max(np.max(wear_true), FAILURE_THRESHOLD) + 28

    phase1 = x_min + 0.14 * (x_max - x_min)
    phase2 = x_min + 0.78 * (x_max - x_min)

    # degradation stages
    ax.axvspan(x_min, phase1, color="#F4E9DA", alpha=0.55, zorder=0)
    ax.axvspan(phase1, phase2, color="#EAF4F0", alpha=0.58, zorder=0)
    ax.axvspan(phase2, x_max, color="#F9E1E5", alpha=0.58, zorder=0)

    # hybrid uncertainty
    ax.fill_between(
        cut,
        hybrid - 2 * band,
        hybrid + 2 * band,
        color=COLORS["unc"],
        alpha=0.16,
        linewidth=0,
        label=r"Hybrid PINDP-Net $\pm 2\sigma$",
        zorder=1
    )

    # curves
    ax.plot(
        cut,
        pure_physics,
        color=COLORS["physics"],
        linewidth=2.4,
        linestyle=(0, (7, 3)),
        label=f"Pure physics",
        zorder=3
    )

    ax.plot(
        cut,
        pure_data,
        color=COLORS["data"],
        linewidth=2.0,
        linestyle=(0, (2, 2)),
        label=f"Pure data",
        zorder=4
    )

    ax.plot(
        cut,
        hybrid,
        color=COLORS["hybrid"],
        linewidth=3.2,
        label=f"Hybrid PINDP-Net",
        zorder=6
    )

    ax.plot(
        cut,
        wear_true,
        color=COLORS["truth"],
        linewidth=1.35,
        marker="o",
        markersize=4.3,
        markerfacecolor="white",
        markeredgewidth=1.1,
        markevery=max(1, len(cut)//28),
        label="Ground truth",
        zorder=7
    )

    # failure threshold
    ax.axhline(
        FAILURE_THRESHOLD,
        color=COLORS["threshold"],
        linewidth=1.8,
        linestyle=(0, (5, 2)),
        zorder=2
    )

    ax.text(
        x_min + 0.60 * (x_max - x_min),
        FAILURE_THRESHOLD + 4,
        "Failure threshold",
        color=COLORS["threshold"],
        fontsize=10.5,
        fontweight="bold"
    )

    # phase labels
    ax.text(
        x_min + 0.035 * (x_max - x_min),
        y_min + 0.12 * (y_max - y_min),
        "Break-in",
        fontsize=10,
        fontstyle="italic",
        color="0.35"
    )

    ax.text(
        x_min + 0.40 * (x_max - x_min),
        y_min + 0.12 * (y_max - y_min),
        "Steady wear",
        fontsize=10,
        fontstyle="italic",
        color="0.35"
    )

    ax.text(
        x_min + 0.81 * (x_max - x_min),
        y_min + 0.27 * (y_max - y_min),
        "Accelerated wear",
        fontsize=10,
        fontstyle="italic",
        color="0.35"
    )

    # labels near curves
    ax.annotate(
        "Pure physics\nsmooth but biased",
        xy=(cut[int(0.62 * len(cut))], pure_physics[int(0.62 * len(cut))]),
        xytext=(0.52, 0.73),
        textcoords="axes fraction",
        arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle="->", color=COLORS["physics"], lw=1.2),
        fontsize=9.2,
        color=COLORS["physics"],
        fontweight="bold"
    )

    ax.annotate(
        "Pure data\nflexible but noisy",
        xy=(cut[int(0.28 * len(cut))], pure_data[int(0.28 * len(cut))]),
        xytext=(0.14, 0.70),
        textcoords="axes fraction",
        arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle="->", color=COLORS["data"], lw=1.2),
        fontsize=9.2,
        color=COLORS["data"],
        fontweight="bold"
    )

    ax.annotate(
        "Hybrid PINDP-Net\naccurate, smooth,\nphysics-consistent",
        xy=(cut[int(0.72 * len(cut))], hybrid[int(0.72 * len(cut))]),
        xytext=(0.58, 0.39),
        textcoords="axes fraction",
        arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle="->", color=COLORS["hybrid"], lw=1.3),
        fontsize=9.2,
        color=COLORS["hybrid"],
        fontweight="bold",
        bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.35", facecolor="white",
                  edgecolor=COLORS["hybrid"], alpha=0.92)
    )

    # metric box
    metric_text = (
        "Performance on measured wear\n"
        f"Pure physics: RMSE={m_phy[0]:.1f}, R²={m_phy[2]:.3f}\n"
        f"Pure data: RMSE={m_data[0]:.1f}, R²={m_data[2]:.3f}\n"
        f"Hybrid: RMSE={m_hyb[0]:.1f}, R²={m_hyb[2]:.3f}"
    )

    ax.text(
        0.035,
        0.965,
        metric_text,
        transform=ax.transAxes,
        va="top",
        ha="left",
        fontsize=8.8,
        bbox=dict(
            boxstyle="round,pad=0.38",
            facecolor="white",
            edgecolor="black",
            linewidth=1.0,
            alpha=0.92
        )
    )

    # axis labels
    ax.set_xlabel("Cutting pass", fontsize=13.5, fontweight="bold")
    ax.set_ylabel("Mean flank wear (μm)", fontsize=13.5, fontweight="bold")

    ax.set_xlim(x_min, x_max)
    ax.set_ylim(y_min, y_max)

    # no global title for MSSP figure style
    ax.grid(True, linestyle=":", linewidth=0.65, alpha=0.35)
    ax.minorticks_on()
    ax.tick_params(axis="both", which="both", top=True, right=True)

    for spine in ax.spines.values():
        spine.set_linewidth(1.35)
        spine.set_color("black")

    for label in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
        label.set_fontweight("bold")

    # legend outside
    leg = ax.legend(
        loc="upper center",
        bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.14),
        ncol=3,
        frameon=True,
        edgecolor="black",
        fontsize=9.2,
        columnspacing=1.2,
        handlelength=2.8
    )

    leg.get_frame().set_linewidth(1.0)

    fig.tight_layout()

    # save
    out_base = SAVE_DIR / f"MSSP_Physics_Data_Hybrid_Comparison_{CUTTER.upper()}"

    fig.savefig(str(out_base) + ".png", dpi=DPI, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
    fig.savefig(str(out_base) + ".pdf", bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")
    fig.savefig(str(out_base) + ".svg", bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white")

    plt.close(fig)

    print("\nSaved:")
    print(str(out_base) + ".png")
    print(str(out_base) + ".pdf")
    print(str(out_base) + ".svg")
